===== src/components/lesson/CourseWorkspace.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { useGamification } from '@/hooks/useGamification';
import { PRACTICE_STAR_TARGET } from '@/lib/gamification';
import GamificationHeader from '@/components/gamification/GamificationHeader';
import ThemePicker from '@/components/layout/ThemePicker';
import TheoryTab from './TheoryTab';
import InteractiveTab from './InteractiveTab';
import HomeworkTab from './HomeworkTab';
import AiTutorTab from './AiTutorTab';
import TopicEditorModal from './TopicEditorModal';
import ScheduleTopicModal from './ScheduleTopicModal';
import BoardDrawOverlay from './BoardDrawOverlay';
import type { BoardViewMode } from './board/boardTypes';
import ConfettiBurst from '@/components/ui/ConfettiBurst';
import { cleanSectionName, getScheduleBadge } from '@/lib/format/cleanTopic';
import { isSoundEnabled, playSound, setSoundEnabled } from '@/lib/audio/sound';
import type {
  ClassScheduleItem,
  Course,
  Material,
  Profile,
  Topic,
} from '@/types/db';

interface ClassroomOption {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  join_code: string;
}

interface Props {
  course: Course;
  initialTopics: Topic[];
  initialCompletedIds: string[];
  profile: Profile | null;
  classrooms?: ClassroomOption[];
  initialSchedules?: ClassScheduleItem[];
  initialTopicId?: string;
  initialTab?: string;
}

type TabId = 'theory' | 'interactive' | 'homework' | 'passport';

const LAST_LESSON_STORAGE_KEY = 'mathhub_last_lesson';

function syncLessonUrl(topicId: string, tab: TabId) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !topicId) return;
  const url = new URL(window.location.href);
  url.searchParams.set('topic', topicId);
  if (tab === 'passport') {
    url.searchParams.delete('tab');
  } else {
    url.searchParams.set('tab', tab);
  }
  const nextHref = `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
  const currentHref = `${window.location.pathname}${window.location.search}${window.location.hash}`;
  if (nextHref !== currentHref) {
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', nextHref);
  }
}

export default function CourseWorkspace({
  course,
  initialTopics,
  initialCompletedIds,
  profile,
  classrooms = [],
  initialSchedules = [],
  initialTopicId,
  initialTab,
}: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const isTeacher = profile?.role === 'teacher';

  const [topics, setTopics] = useState<Topic[]>(initialTopics);
  const [schedules, setSchedules] = useState<ClassScheduleItem[]>(initialSchedules);
  const [teacherMode, setTeacherMode] = useState(false);
  const [teacherMenuOpen, setTeacherMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [boardMode, setBoardMode] = useState(false);
  const [boardLayout, setBoardLayout] = useState<BoardViewMode>('glass');
  const [tutorOpen, setTutorOpen] = useState(false);
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [soundOn, setSoundOn] = useState(true);
  const [confettiKey, setConfettiKey] = useState(0);

  const {
    stats,
    starsMap,
    rewardBanner,
    markSlidesDone,
    reportPracticeSolved,
    markHomeworkDone,
  } = useGamification(Boolean(profile));

  const initialMatchedTopic =
    initialTopics.find((t) => t.id === initialTopicId) ||
    initialTopics[0] ||
    null;

  const [activeQuarter, setActiveQuarter] = useState<number>(
    initialMatchedTopic?.quarter || 1
  );
  const [activeTopicId, setActiveTopicId] = useState<string>(
    initialMatchedTopic?.id || ''
  );
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabId>(() => {
    if (initialTab === 'interactive' || initialTab === 'homework') {
      return initialTab;
    }
    return 'theory';
  });

  // Локальная отметка пройденных слайдов (работает и для гостей)
  const [slidesDoneIds, setSlidesDoneIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'assessment' | 'completed'>('all');
  const [completedIds, setCompletedIds] = useState<string[]>(initialCompletedIds);
  const [editingTopicModal, setEditingTopicModal] = useState(false);
  const [scheduleModalOpen, setScheduleModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<Material[]>([]);
  const [notesDraft, setNotesDraft] = useState('');
  const [newTopicTitle, setNewTopicTitle] = useState('');
  const [newTopicSection, setNewTopicSection] = useState('');

  const tabsNavRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const teacherMenuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSoundOn(isSoundEnabled());

    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (
        teacherMenuRef.current &&
        !teacherMenuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)
      ) {
        setTeacherMenuOpen(false);
      }
    }
    function handleEsc(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setDrawerOpen(false);
        setTeacherMenuOpen(false);
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
    };
  }, []);

  // Синхронизация темы и вкладки при переходе с экрана «Сегодня» или «Карты тем»
  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialTopicId) {
      const matched = initialTopics.find((t) => t.id === initialTopicId);
      if (matched) {
        setActiveTopicId(matched.id);
        setActiveQuarter(matched.quarter);
      }
    }
    if (
      initialTab === 'interactive' ||
      initialTab === 'homework' ||
      initialTab === 'theory'
    ) {
      setActiveTab(initialTab);
      if (initialTab !== 'theory') {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
          tabsNavRef.current?.scrollIntoView({
            behavior: 'smooth',
            block: 'start',
          });
        }, 180);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }
  }, [initialTopicId, initialTab, initialTopics]);

  function toggleSound() {
    const next = !soundOn;
    setSoundEnabled(next);
    setSoundOn(next);
    if (next) playSound('click');
  }

  const orderedTopics = useMemo(
    () => [...topics].sort((a, b) => a.order_index - b.order_index),
    [topics]
  );

  const activeTopic = useMemo(
    () =>
      orderedTopics.find((t) => t.id === activeTopicId) ||
      orderedTopics[0] ||
      null,
    [orderedTopics, activeTopicId]
  );

  const activeIndex = activeTopic
    ? orderedTopics.findIndex((t) => t.id === activeTopic.id)
    : -1;
  const prevTopic = activeIndex > 0 ? orderedTopics[activeIndex - 1] : null;
  const nextTopic =
    activeIndex >= 0 && activeIndex < orderedTopics.length - 1
      ? orderedTopics[activeIndex + 1]
      : null;

  const topicBadgeMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, 'today' | 'tomorrow'>();
    for (const s of schedules) {
      const b = getScheduleBadge(s.lesson_date);
      if (b === 'today') {
        map.set(s.topic_id, 'today');
      } else if (b === 'tomorrow' && map.get(s.topic_id) !== 'today') {
        map.set(s.topic_id, 'tomorrow');
      }
    }
    return map;
  }, [schedules]);

  // Обновляем URL и запоминаем последний открытый урок для экрана «Сегодня»
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeTopic) return;
    syncLessonUrl(activeTopic.id, activeTab);
    try {
      const safeTab = activeTab === 'passport' ? 'theory' : activeTab;
      window.localStorage.setItem(
        LAST_LESSON_STORAGE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({
          courseId: course.id,
          courseTitle: course.title,
          pathname: window.location.pathname,
          topicId: activeTopic.id,
          topicTitle: activeTopic.title,
          orderIndex: activeTopic.order_index,
          quarter: activeTopic.quarter,
          tab: safeTab,
          updatedAt: Date.now(),
        })
      );
    } catch {
      // Игнорируем ограничения приватного режима
    }
  }, [
    activeTopic?.id,
    activeTopic?.title,
    activeTopic?.order_index,
    activeTopic?.quarter,
    activeTab,
    course.id,
    course.title,
  ]);

  function goToTopic(topic: Topic) {
    playSound('click');
    setActiveTopicId(topic.id);
    setActiveQuarter(topic.quarter);
    setActiveTab('theory');
    syncLessonUrl(topic.id, 'theory');
    setDrawerOpen(false);
    setTutorOpen(false);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  function switchTab(nextTab: TabId, scrollToTabs = false) {
    playSound('click');
    setActiveTab(nextTab);
    if (activeTopic) {
      syncLessonUrl(activeTopic.id, nextTab);
    }
    if (scrollToTabs) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        tabsNavRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      }, 60);
    }
  }

  function toggleBoardMode() {
    setBoardMode((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      if (next) {
        setTeacherMode(false);
        setDrawerOpen(false);
        setTutorOpen(false);
        setTeacherMenuOpen(false);
      } else {
        setBoardLayout('glass');
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeTopic) return;
    setNotesDraft(activeTopic.teacher_notes || '');
    let cancelled = false;
    supabase
      .from('materials')
      .select('*')
      .eq('topic_id', activeTopic.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
      .then(({ data }) => {
        if (!cancelled && data) setMaterials(data as Material[]);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [activeTopic?.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!teacherMode && activeTab === 'passport') {
      setActiveTab('theory');
    }
  }, [teacherMode, activeTab]);

  const groupedTopics = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    const list = orderedTopics.filter((t) => {
      if (!q && t.quarter !== activeQuarter) return false;
      if (filter === 'assessment' && !t.is_assessment) return false;
      if (filter === 'completed' && !completedIds.includes(t.id)) return false;
      if (q) {
        const objs = Array.isArray(t.objectives) ? t.objectives.join(' ') : '';
        const hay = `${t.title} ${t.section} ${objs}`.toLowerCase();
        return hay.includes(q);
      }
      return true;
    });

    const map = new Map<string, Topic[]>();
    list.forEach((t) => {
      const secName = teacherMode ? t.section : cleanSectionName(t.section);
      if (!map.has(secName)) map.set(secName, []);
      map.get(secName)!.push(t);
    });
    return map;
  }, [orderedTopics, activeQuarter, search, filter, completedIds, teacherMode]);

  const progressPercent = topics.length
    ? Math.round((completedIds.length / topics.length) * 100)
    : 0;

  async function autoCompleteTopic(topicId: string) {
    if (!profile || completedIds.includes(topicId)) return;
    setCompletedIds((prev) => (prev.includes(topicId) ? prev : [...prev, topicId]));
    playSound('celebrate');
    setConfettiKey((k) => k + 1);
    await supabase.from('topic_progress').upsert({
      student_id: profile.id,
      topic_id: topicId,
      completed: true,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  async function toggleComplete(topicId: string) {
    if (!profile) {
      alert('Войди в аккаунт, чтобы сохранять прогресс.');
      return;
    }
    const nextDone = !completedIds.includes(topicId);
    if (nextDone) {
      playSound('celebrate');
      setConfettiKey((k) => k + 1);
    } else {
      playSound('click');
    }
    setCompletedIds((prev) =>
      nextDone ? [...prev, topicId] : prev.filter((id) => id !== topicId)
    );
    await supabase.from('topic_progress').upsert({
      student_id: profile.id,
      topic_id: topicId,
      completed: nextDone,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  function handlePracticeSolved(totalSolved: number) {
    if (!activeTopic) return;
    const current = starsMap[activeTopic.id];
    const becameDone = !current?.practice_done && totalSolved >= PRACTICE_STAR_TARGET;
    if (becameDone) {
      setConfettiKey((k) => k + 1);
    }
    reportPracticeSolved(activeTopic.id, totalSolved);

    const hasSlides =
      Boolean(current?.slides_done) || slidesDoneIds.includes(activeTopic.id);
    const hasPractice =
      Boolean(current?.practice_done) || totalSolved >= PRACTICE_STAR_TARGET;
    const hasHomework = Boolean(current?.homework_done);
    if (hasSlides && hasPractice && hasHomework) {
      void autoCompleteTopic(activeTopic.id);
    }
  }

  function handleSlidesCompleted() {
    if (!activeTopic) return;
    const current = starsMap[activeTopic.id];
    if (!current?.slides_done) {
      setConfettiKey((k) => k + 1);
    }
    setSlidesDoneIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(activeTopic.id) ? prev : [...prev, activeTopic.id]
    );
    markSlidesDone(activeTopic.id);

    if (current?.practice_done && current?.homework_done) {
      void autoCompleteTopic(activeTopic.id);
    }
  }

  function handleHomeworkDone() {
    if (!activeTopic) return;
    const current = starsMap[activeTopic.id];
    if (!current?.homework_done) {
      setConfettiKey((k) => k + 1);
    }
    markHomeworkDone(activeTopic.id);

    const hasSlides =
      Boolean(current?.slides_done) || slidesDoneIds.includes(activeTopic.id);
    if (hasSlides && current?.practice_done) {
      void autoCompleteTopic(activeTopic.id);
    }
  }

  async function handleAddTopic(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newTopicTitle.trim()) return;
    const nextOrder = topics.length
      ? Math.max(...topics.map((t) => t.order_index)) + 1
      : 1;

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('topics')
      .insert({
        course_id: course.id,
        order_index: nextOrder,
        quarter: activeQuarter,
        section:
          newTopicSection.trim() || `Раздел ${activeQuarter} четверти`,
        title: newTopicTitle.trim(),
        theory_md: `### ${newTopicTitle.trim()}\nДобавьте конспект урока в режиме учителя.`,
      })
      .select()
      .single();

    if (!error && data) {
      const created = data as Topic;
      setTopics((prev) => [...prev, created]);
      setActiveTopicId(created.id);
      setNewTopicTitle('');
      setDrawerOpen(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveTeacherNotes() {
    if (!activeTopic) return;
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('topics')
      .update({ teacher_notes: notesDraft })
      .eq('id', activeTopic.id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (data) {
      setTopics((prev) =>
        prev.map((t) => (t.id === activeTopic.id ? (data as Topic) : t))
      );
      playSound('success');
      alert('Заметки сохранены!');
    }
  }

  const activeObjectives = Array.isArray(activeTopic?.objectives)
    ? activeTopic.objectives
    : [];
  const activeScheduleBadge = activeTopic
    ? topicBadgeMap.get(activeTopic.id)
    : undefined;

  const activeStars = activeTopic ? starsMap[activeTopic.id] : undefined;
  const isTopicDone = activeTopic ? completedIds.includes(activeTopic.id) : false;

  // Каждая звезда зажигается честно по своему шагу (без автоподстановки от isTopicDone)
  const slidesStar = activeTopic
    ? Boolean(activeStars?.slides_done) || slidesDoneIds.includes(activeTopic.id)
    : false;
  const practiceStar = Boolean(activeStars?.practice_done);
  const homeworkStar = Boolean(activeStars?.homework_done);

  const solvedCount = Math.min(
    PRACTICE_STAR_TARGET,
    Number(activeStars?.practice_solved || 0)
  );
  const earnedStarsCount =
    (slidesStar ? 1 : 0) + (practiceStar ? 1 : 0) + (homeworkStar ? 1 : 0);

  const showStarSubs = !boardMode && !teacherMode && Boolean(profile);

  const tabs: {
    id: TabId;
    icon: string;
    label: string;
    shortLabel: string;
    sub?: string;
    star?: boolean;
  }[] = teacherMode
    ? [
        {
          id: 'theory',
          icon: '📘',
          label: '1. Понять',
          shortLabel: 'Понять',
          star: slidesStar,
        },
        {
          id: 'interactive',
          icon: '🎮',
          label: '2. Потренироваться',
          shortLabel: 'Тренажёр',
          star: practiceStar,
        },
        {
          id: 'homework',
          icon: '📝',
          label: '3. Сдать ДЗ',
          shortLabel: 'ДЗ',
          star: homeworkStar,
        },
        {
          id: 'passport',
          icon: '📋',
          label: 'Паспорт КТП',
          shortLabel: 'КТП',
        },
      ]
    : [
        {
          id: 'theory',
          icon: '📘',
          label: '1. Понять',
          shortLabel: 'Понять',
          sub: slidesStar ? 'Пройдено' : 'Дойди до конца',
          star: slidesStar,
        },
        {
          id: 'interactive',
          icon: '🎮',
          label: '2. Потренироваться',
          shortLabel: 'Тренажёр',
          sub: practiceStar
            ? 'Зачтено'
            : `Решено ${solvedCount} из ${PRACTICE_STAR_TARGET}`,
          star: practiceStar,
        },
        {
          id: 'homework',
          icon: '📝',
          label: '3. Сдать ДЗ',
          shortLabel: 'ДЗ',
          sub: homeworkStar ? 'Сдано' : 'Отправь ответы',
          star: homeworkStar,
        },
      ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
      <ConfettiBurst burstKey={confettiKey} />

      {/* Чистая эргономичная шапка */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-200/80">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-5 h-16 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            {!boardMode && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  playSound('click');
                  setDrawerOpen(true);
                }}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-50 text-slate-700 hover:text-indigo-700 text-xs font-extrabold border border-slate-200 transition shrink-0"
                title="Открыть список всех уроков курса"
              >
                <span>🗺️</span>
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">Все темы</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <Link
              href="/today"
              className="w-9 h-9 sm:w-10 sm:h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 via-violet-500 to-fuchsia-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-extrabold text-sm sm:text-base shrink-0 shadow-xs"
              title="На экран «Сегодня»"
            >
              ∑{course.grade_number}
            </Link>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h1 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900 truncate">
                {course.title}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Link
                  href="/today"
                  className="text-indigo-600 font-bold hover:underline"
                >
                  Сегодня
                </Link>
                <span>·</span>
                <Link href="/courses" className="hover:underline font-semibold">
                  Карта тем
                </Link>
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
            {/* Выбор стиля оформления */}
            <ThemePicker compact />

            {/* Звук */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={toggleSound}
              title={soundOn ? 'Выключить звуки' : 'Включить звуки'}
              className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-center text-sm transition ${
                soundOn
                  ? 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:border-indigo-300'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 border-slate-200 text-slate-400'
              }`}
            >
              {soundOn ? '🔊' : '🔇'}
            </button>

            {/* Режим проектора / доски */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={toggleBoardMode}
              title="Режим проектора с маркером"
              className={`px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap transition ${
                boardMode
                  ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900'
                  : 'bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200'
              }`}
            >
              {boardMode ? '✕ Обычный вид' : '🖥️ Доска'}
            </button>

            {/* Компактное меню учителя */}
            {isTeacher && !boardMode && (
              <div className="relative" ref={teacherMenuRef}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTeacherMenuOpen((v) => !v)}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                    teacherMode || teacherMenuOpen
                      ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-700 shadow-xs'
                      : 'bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 border-indigo-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>{teacherMode ? '🛠️' : '👨‍🏫'}</span>
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">
                    {teacherMode ? 'Редактор вкл' : 'Учитель'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px]">▾</span>
                </button>

                {teacherMenuOpen && (
                  <div className="theme-popover absolute right-0 mt-2 w-60 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl p-1.5 space-y-1 z-50 animate-pop">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setTeacherMode((v) => !v);
                        setTeacherMenuOpen(false);
                      }}
                      className={`w-full text-left flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                        teacherMode
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                          : 'hover:bg-indigo-50 text-slate-800'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span>🛠️ Режим редактора</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] opacity-80">
                        {teacherMode ? 'ВКЛ' : 'ВЫКЛ'}
                      </span>
                    </button>

                    {activeTopic && (
                      <>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setScheduleModalOpen(true);
                            setTeacherMenuOpen(false);
                          }}
                          className="w-full text-left flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-emerald-50 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800"
                        >
                          <span>📅</span>
                          <span>Назначить урок классу</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setEditingTopicModal(true);
                            setTeacherMenuOpen(false);
                          }}
                          className="w-full text-left flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800"
                        >
                          <span>⚙️</span>
                          <span>Настроить тему и симулятор</span>
                        </button>
                      </>
                    )}

                    <div className="border-t border-slate-100 my-1" />

                    <Link
                      href="/teacher/journal"
                      onClick={() => setTeacherMenuOpen(false)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-indigo-50 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800"
                    >
                      <span>📊</span>
                      <span>Журнал и PIN-карточки</span>
                    </Link>
                    <Link
                      href="/teacher/submissions"
                      onClick={() => setTeacherMenuOpen(false)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-amber-50 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800"
                    >
                      <span>📥</span>
                      <span>Проверка ДЗ по классам</span>
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {!profile && !boardMode && (
              <Link
                href="/login"
                className="btn-tactile px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 text-white text-xs font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                Войти
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Выезжающая шторка «Все темы» */}
      {!boardMode && drawerOpen && (
        <div
          onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex"
        >
          <aside
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="w-[310px] sm:w-[350px] max-w-[90vw] h-full bg-white border-r border-slate-200 shadow-2xl flex flex-col animate-pop"
          >
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-100 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                  🗺️ Все темы курса
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}
                  className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-extrabold text-xs"
                >
                  ✕
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl p-3 border border-slate-100 space-y-1.5">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Прогресс по курсу</span>
                  <span className="text-emerald-600 font-extrabold tabular-nums">
                    {progressPercent}%
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="w-full h-2 bg-slate-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full transition-all"
                    style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
                  Пройдено {completedIds.length} из {topics.length} уроков
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
                {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((q) => (
                  <button
                    key={q}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveQuarter(q)}
                    className={`py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold border transition ${
                      activeQuarter === q
                        ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600'
                        : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {q} ч.
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={
                  teacherMode ? 'Поиск темы или кода ЦО...' : 'Найти тему...'
                }
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
              />

              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                {(
                  [
                    ['all', 'Все'],
                    ['assessment', 'СОР / СОЧ'],
                    ['completed', 'Пройденные'],
                  ] as const
                ).map(([id, label]) => (
                  <button
                    key={id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setFilter(id)}
                    className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg transition ${
                      filter === id
                        ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                        : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-3">
              {Array.from(groupedTopics.entries()).map(([section, secTopics]) => (
                <details key={section} open className="group">
                  <summary className="cursor-pointer list-none px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-800 flex justify-between">
                    <span className="truncate">{section}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 ml-2">
                      {secTopics.length}
                    </span>
                  </summary>
                  <div className="mt-1.5 space-y-1 pl-1">
                    {secTopics.map((t) => {
                      const isActive = t.id === activeTopic?.id;
                      const isDone = completedIds.includes(t.id);
                      const badge = topicBadgeMap.get(t.id);
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={t.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => goToTopic(t)}
                          className={`w-full text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl border transition flex items-start gap-2 ${
                            isActive
                              ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-400'
                              : badge === 'today'
                                ? 'bg-emerald-50/70 border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-50'
                                : badge === 'tomorrow'
                                  ? 'bg-sky-50/60 border-sky-200 hover:bg-sky-50'
                                  : 'bg-white border-transparent hover:bg-slate-50'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <span
                            className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-extrabold shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${
                              isDone
                                ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white'
                                : badge === 'today'
                                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                                  : isActive
                                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                                    : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {isDone ? '✓' : t.order_index}
                          </span>
                          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 leading-snug">
                              {t.title}
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mt-1">
                              {badge === 'today' && (
                                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[9px] font-extrabold">
                                  ● Сегодня
                                </span>
                              )}
                              {badge === 'tomorrow' && (
                                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-sky-500 text-white text-[9px] font-extrabold">
                                  Завтра
                                </span>
                              )}
                              {t.is_assessment && (
                                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[9px] font-extrabold">
                                  🏆 СОР/СОЧ
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </details>
              ))}

              {teacherMode && (
                <form
                  onSubmit={handleAddTopic}
                  className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 space-y-2"
                >
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-indigo-800">
                    + Новая тема ({activeQuarter} ч.)
                  </div>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={newTopicSection}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTopicSection(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Раздел (напр. 5.1А Натуральные числа)"
                    className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={newTopicTitle}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewTopicTitle(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Название темы урока"
                    className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="w-full py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-extrabold"
                  >
                    Добавить тему
                  </button>
                </form>
              )}
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Просторная центральная зона урока */}
      <main
        className={`flex-1 w-full px-3 sm:px-6 pt-4 pb-28 transition-all ${
          boardMode && boardLayout === 'split' ? 'md:pr-[51vw]' : ''
        }`}
      >
        {!activeTopic ? (
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center space-y-3">
            <div className="text-4xl">📚</div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">
              В этом курсе пока нет тем
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
              Открой «👨‍🏫 Учитель → Режим редактора» и добавь первую тему через «🗺️ Все темы».
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div
            className={`mx-auto space-y-3.5 ${
              boardMode ? 'max-w-7xl' : 'max-w-5xl'
            }`}
          >
            {/* Панель геймификации: уровень, XP, стрик и награды */}
            {!boardMode && !teacherMode && profile && (
              <GamificationHeader stats={stats} rewardBanner={rewardBanner} />
            )}

            {/* Живая компактная карточка урока */}
            <section
              className={`bg-white rounded-3xl border-2 shadow-xs p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3 ${
                activeScheduleBadge === 'today'
                  ? 'border-emerald-400'
                  : activeScheduleBadge === 'tomorrow'
                    ? 'border-sky-300'
                    : 'border-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1.5 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                    {activeScheduleBadge === 'today' && (
                      <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 text-white font-extrabold">
                        ● Урок сегодня
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {activeScheduleBadge === 'tomorrow' && (
                      <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-sky-500 text-white font-extrabold">
                        Завтра
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 font-bold text-slate-700">
                      {activeTopic.quarter} четверть
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-indigo-600">
                      {teacherMode
                        ? activeTopic.section
                        : cleanSectionName(activeTopic.section)}
                    </span>
                    {activeTopic.is_assessment && (
                      <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 font-extrabold">
                        🏆 СОР / СОЧ
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <h2
                    className={`font-extrabold text-slate-900 leading-tight ${
                      boardMode ? 'text-2xl sm:text-3xl' : 'text-lg sm:text-2xl'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {activeTopic.order_index}. {activeTopic.title}
                  </h2>
                </div>

                {/* Кнопка завершения урока */}
                {!boardMode && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleComplete(activeTopic.id)}
                      className={`btn-tactile px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold ${
                        isTopicDone
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-700 text-white'
                          : 'bg-slate-900 hover:bg-indigo-600 border-slate-950 text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isTopicDone ? '✓ Урок пройден' : '○ Отметить пройденным'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {activeTopic.value_example && (
                <div
                  className={`px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-200/80 text-indigo-900 ${
                    boardMode ? 'text-sm sm:text-base' : 'text-xs sm:text-sm'
                  }`}
                >
                  <strong>💡 Задача из жизни:</strong> {activeTopic.value_example}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            {/* Объединённая квест-панель: 3 шага урока + звёзды прогресса */}
            <nav
              ref={tabsNavRef}
              className="scroll-mt-20 bg-white p-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <div
                className={`grid gap-1.5 flex-1 ${
                  teacherMode ? 'grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4' : 'grid-cols-3'
                }`}
              >
                {tabs.map((tab) => {
                  const active = activeTab === tab.id;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={tab.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => switchTab(tab.id)}
                      className={`px-2.5 sm:px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center sm:justify-start gap-2 text-left ${
                        active
                          ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                          : tab.star
                            ? 'bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 text-amber-950 hover:border-amber-300'
                            : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="text-base leading-none shrink-0">
                        {tab.star ? '⭐' : tab.icon}
                      </span>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold truncate leading-tight">
                          <span className="sm:hidden">{tab.shortLabel}</span>
                          <span className="hidden sm:inline">{tab.label}</span>
                        </div>
                        {showStarSubs && tab.sub && (
                          <div
                            className={`text-[10px] font-semibold truncate hidden sm:block mt-0.5 ${
                              active ? 'text-white/80' : 'opacity-70'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {tab.sub}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {showStarSubs && (
                <div
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 sm:py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold text-center shrink-0 tabular-nums ${
                    earnedStarsCount === 3
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-orange-500 text-white shadow-xs'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'
                  }`}
                  title="Звёзды, собранные в этом уроке"
                >
                  {earnedStarsCount === 3
                    ? '🏆 3 / 3'
                    : `⭐ ${earnedStarsCount} / 3`}
                </div>
              )}
            </nav>

            {/* Содержимое активного шага */}
            {activeTab === 'theory' && (
              <TheoryTab
                topic={activeTopic}
                materials={materials}
                canEdit={teacherMode}
                boardMode={boardMode}
                onTopicUpdated={(upd) =>
                  setTopics((prev) =>
                    prev.map((t) => (t.id === upd.id ? upd : t))
                  )
                }
                onMaterialsChanged={setMaterials}
                onSlidesCompleted={handleSlidesCompleted}
                onGoToPractice={() => switchTab('interactive', true)}
              />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'interactive' && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <InteractiveTab
                  topic={activeTopic}
                  materials={materials}
                  canEdit={teacherMode}
                  profile={profile}
                  isTopicCompleted={isTopicDone}
                  onMaterialsChanged={setMaterials}
                  onProblemSolved={handlePracticeSolved}
                />

                {!boardMode && (
                  <div className="rounded-3xl bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
                      Потренировался? Самое время проверить себя в домашнем задании!
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => switchTab('homework', true)}
                      className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 border-amber-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold"
                    >
                      📝 Перейти к ДЗ →
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'homework' && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <HomeworkTab
                  topic={activeTopic}
                  canEdit={teacherMode}
                  profile={profile}
                  onHomeworkSubmitted={handleHomeworkDone}
                />

                {!boardMode && !isTopicDone && (
                  <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-50 to-teal-50 border border-emerald-200 p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-emerald-900">
                      Всё разобрал и отправил ДЗ? Отметь урок пройденным!
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleComplete(activeTopic.id)}
                      className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold"
                    >
                      ⭐ Завершить урок
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'passport' && teacherMode && (
              <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 space-y-5">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                    Цели обучения (ЦО)
                  </h3>
                  <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
                    {activeObjectives.map((o, i) => (
                      <li
                        key={i}
                        className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200"
                      >
                        🎯 {o}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-slate-500">
                    Методические заметки учителя
                  </h4>
                  <textarea
                    rows={4}
                    value={notesDraft}
                    onChange={(e) => setNotesDraft(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-3 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={saveTeacherNotes}
                    className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 text-white text-xs font-extrabold"
                  >
                    💾 Сохранить заметки
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Удобная навигация между уроками */}
            {(prevTopic || nextTopic) && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2">
                {prevTopic ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => goToTopic(prevTopic)}
                    className="card-lift text-left p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-400 hover:shadow-xs transition group"
                  >
                    <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 group-hover:text-indigo-600">
                      ← Предыдущий урок
                      {prevTopic.quarter !== activeTopic.quarter
                        ? ` (${prevTopic.quarter} четв.)`
                        : ''}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-800 mt-0.5 line-clamp-1">
                      {prevTopic.order_index}. {prevTopic.title}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <div />
                )}

                {nextTopic && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => goToTopic(nextTopic)}
                    className="card-lift text-right p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-400 hover:shadow-xs transition group"
                  >
                    <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 group-hover:text-indigo-600">
                      Следующий урок
                      {nextTopic.quarter !== activeTopic.quarter
                        ? ` (${nextTopic.quarter} четв.)`
                        : ''}{' '}
                      →
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-800 mt-0.5 line-clamp-1">
                      {nextTopic.order_index}. {nextTopic.title}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Живая кнопка совы Сократа */}
      {!boardMode && activeTopic && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            playSound('click');
            setTutorOpen(true);
          }}
          className={`fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-40 btn-tactile px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold ${
            activeTopic.is_assessment
              ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border-amber-300'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 hover:from-indigo-700 hover:to-violet-700 text-white border-indigo-900'
          }`}
        >
          <span className="text-lg leading-none animate-float">
            {activeTopic.is_assessment ? '🔒' : '🦉'}
          </span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">
            {activeTopic.is_assessment
              ? 'СОР / СОЧ'
              : activeTab === 'homework'
                ? 'Подсказка от Сократа'
                : 'Спросить Сократа'}
          </span>
        </button>
      )}

      {/* Окно ИИ-тьютора «Сократ» */}
      {tutorOpen && activeTopic && (
        <div
          onClick={() => setTutorOpen(false)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto"
        >
          <div
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="w-full max-w-3xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-3xl relative animate-pop"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTutorOpen(false)}
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-bold"
              title="Закрыть"
            >
              ✕
            </button>
            <AiTutorTab
              topic={activeTopic}
              profile={profile}
              onGoToTheory={() => {
                setActiveTab('theory');
                setTutorOpen(false);
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {boardMode && (
        <BoardDrawOverlay
          onExitBoard={() => {
            setBoardMode(false);
            setBoardLayout('glass');
          }}
          topic={activeTopic}
          isTeacher={isTeacher}
          onMaterialSaved={(created) =>
            setMaterials((prev) => [...prev, created])
          }
          onLayoutChange={setBoardLayout}
        />
      )}

      {scheduleModalOpen && activeTopic && (
        <ScheduleTopicModal
          topic={activeTopic}
          classrooms={classrooms}
          schedules={schedules}
          onClose={() => setScheduleModalOpen(false)}
          onSaved={(item) => {
            setSchedules((prev) => {
              const filtered = prev.filter(
                (s) =>
                  !(
                    s.classroom_id === item.classroom_id &&
                    s.topic_id === item.topic_id
                  )
              );
              return [...filtered, item];
            });
          }}
          onDeleted={(classroomId, topicId) => {
            setSchedules((prev) =>
              prev.filter(
                (s) =>
                  !(s.classroom_id === classroomId && s.topic_id === topicId)
              )
            );
          }}
        />
      )}

      {editingTopicModal && activeTopic && (
        <TopicEditorModal
          topic={activeTopic}
          onClose={() => setEditingTopicModal(false)}
          onUpdated={(upd) => {
            setTopics((prev) =>
              prev
                .map((t) => (t.id === upd.id ? upd : t))
                .sort((a, b) => a.order_index - b.order_index)
            );
            setActiveQuarter(upd.quarter);
          }}
          onDeleted={(deletedId) => {
            setTopics((prev) => {
              const rest = prev.filter((t) => t.id !== deletedId);
              setActiveTopicId(rest[0]?.id || '');
              return rest;
            });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/HomeworkTab.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import MathMarkdown from './MathMarkdown';
import { playSound } from '@/lib/audio/sound';
import { compressImageFile } from '@/lib/media/compressImage';
import type { HomeworkTask, Profile, Submission, Topic } from '@/types/db';
import MathInput from '@/components/ui/MathInput';
interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  canEdit: boolean;
  profile: Profile | null;
  onHomeworkSubmitted?: () => void;
}

interface DraftGenTask {
  type: 'test' | 'input' | 'open';
  difficulty: 'base' | 'standard' | 'pro';
  points: number;
  question_md: string;
  options: string[];
  correct_answer: string;
  solution_hint: string;
}

interface ExamSession {
  startedAt: number;
  endsAt: number;
  finishedAt?: number;
}

const DIFF_META: Record<HomeworkTask['difficulty'], { label: string; cls: string }> = {
  base: { label: 'База', cls: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200' },
  standard: { label: 'Стандарт', cls: 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200' },
  pro: { label: 'Профи', cls: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200' },
};

// Детерминированное перемешивание (одинаковое при F5, разное у каждого ученика)
function hashString(str: string): number {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

function seededShuffle<T>(items: T[], seed: number): T[] {
  const arr = [...items];
  let s = seed >>> 0;
  for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    s = (s + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = Math.imul(s ^ (s >>> 15), 1 | s);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    const rnd = ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    const j = Math.floor(rnd * (i + 1));
    [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
  }
  return arr;
}

function formatCountdown(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const safe = Math.max(0, Math.floor(totalSeconds));
  const mins = Math.floor(safe / 60);
  const secs = safe % 60;
  return `${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function getDraftStorageKey(userId: string | undefined, topicId: string): string {
  return `mathhub_hw_drafts_${userId || 'guest'}_${topicId}`;
}

function getExamStorageKey(userId: string | undefined, topicId: string): string {
  return `mathhub_exam_session_${userId || 'guest'}_${topicId}`;
}

function readLocalDrafts(storageKey: string): Record<string, string> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {};
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') return {};
    const result: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
      if (typeof v === 'string') result[k] = v;
    }
    return result;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function writeLocalDrafts(storageKey: string, drafts: Record<string, string>): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    if (Object.keys(drafts).length === 0) {
      window.localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
    } else {
      window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(drafts));
    }
  } catch {
    // Игнорируем ограничения приватного режима или переполнение квоты
  }
}

function readExamSession(storageKey: string): ExamSession | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<ExamSession>;
    if (
      !parsed ||
      typeof parsed.startedAt !== 'number' ||
      typeof parsed.endsAt !== 'number'
    ) {
      return null;
    }
    return {
      startedAt: parsed.startedAt,
      endsAt: parsed.endsAt,
      finishedAt:
        typeof parsed.finishedAt === 'number' ? parsed.finishedAt : undefined,
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function writeExamSession(storageKey: string, session: ExamSession): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(session));
  } catch {
    // Игнорируем ограничения приватного режима
  }
}

function HomeworkShortInput({
  value,
  busy,
  examMode = false,
  disabled = false,
  onChange,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  value: string;
  busy: boolean;
  examMode?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onChange: (next: string) => void;
  onSubmit: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!examMode && !disabled) onSubmit();
      }}
      className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 max-w-md"
    >
      <MathInput
        value={value}
        disabled={disabled || busy}
        examMode={examMode}
        submitLabel="✓ Проверить"
        onChange={onChange}
        onSubmit={onSubmit}
        placeholder="Введи ответ (напр. 42 или 3/4)..."
        className="px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-60"
      />
      {!examMode && (
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={busy || disabled}
          className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition shrink-0"
        >
          {busy ? 'Проверка...' : 'Проверить'}
        </button>
      )}
    </form>
  );
}

export default function HomeworkTab({
  topic,
  canEdit,
  profile,
  onHomeworkSubmitted,
}: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const profileId = profile?.id;
  const isTeacher = profile?.role === 'teacher';
  const draftStorageKey = getDraftStorageKey(profileId, topic.id);
  const examStorageKey = getExamStorageKey(profileId, topic.id);

  // Режим СОР/СОЧ с перемешиванием и таймером активен только для учеников
  const isStudentExam = Boolean(topic.is_assessment && !canEdit && !isTeacher && profile);
  const examDurationMin = /соч/i.test(topic.title) ? 40 : 20;
  const examVariant = useMemo(() => {
    if (!profileId) return 'Вариант А';
    return hashString(`${profileId}:${topic.id}`) % 2 === 0
      ? 'Вариант А'
      : 'Вариант Б';
  }, [profileId, topic.id]);

  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<HomeworkTask[]>([]);
  const [submissions, setSubmissions] = useState<Record<string, Submission>>({});
  const [photoUrls, setPhotoUrls] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [draftAnswers, setDraftAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [unsavedDrafts, setUnsavedDrafts] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [hints, setHints] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [busyTaskId, setBusyTaskId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Состояние экзамена СОР / СОЧ
  const [examSession, setExamSession] = useState<ExamSession | null>(null);
  const [remainingSec, setRemainingSec] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [submittingExam, setSubmittingExam] = useState(false);
  const [examAutoExpired, setExamAutoExpired] = useState(false);

  // Поля ручного конструктора ДЗ
  const [hwType, setHwType] = useState<'test' | 'input' | 'open'>('test');
  const [difficulty, setDifficulty] = useState<'base' | 'standard' | 'pro'>('standard');
  const [points, setPoints] = useState(2);
  const [questionMd, setQuestionMd] = useState('');
  const [optionsText, setOptionsText] = useState('');
  const [correctAnswer, setCorrectAnswer] = useState('');
  const [solutionHint, setSolutionHint] = useState('');
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  // Поля ИИ-генератора ДЗ
  const [aiCount, setAiCount] = useState<3 | 5>(3);
  const [aiWish, setAiWish] = useState('');
  const [aiLoading, setAiLoading] = useState(false);
  const [aiError, setAiError] = useState('');
  const [aiDrafts, setAiDrafts] = useState<DraftGenTask[]>([]);
  const [savingDrafts, setSavingDrafts] = useState(false);

  const draftAnswersRef = useRef(draftAnswers);
  const submissionsRef = useRef(submissions);
  const tasksRef = useRef(tasks);
  const finishLockRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    draftAnswersRef.current = draftAnswers;
  }, [draftAnswers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    submissionsRef.current = submissions;
  }, [submissions]);

  useEffect(() => {
    tasksRef.current = tasks;
  }, [tasks]);

  // Перемешиваем задания и варианты тестов индивидуально для ученика на СОР/СОЧ
  const displayTasks = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isStudentExam || !profileId) return tasks;
    const topicSeed = hashString(`${profileId}:${topic.id}`);
    return seededShuffle(tasks, topicSeed).map((t) =>
      t.type === 'test' && t.options.length > 1
        ? {
            ...t,
            options: seededShuffle(t.options, hashString(`${profileId}:${t.id}`)),
          }
        : t
    );
  }, [isStudentExam, profileId, tasks, topic.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    finishLockRef.current = false;

    async function load() {
      setLoading(true);
      setAiDrafts([]);
      setAiError('');
      setExamAutoExpired(false);

      try {
        const { data: taskRows } = await supabase
          .from('homework_tasks')
          .select('*')
          .eq('topic_id', topic.id)
          .order('order_index', { ascending: true });

        const loadedTasks = (taskRows as HomeworkTask[]) || [];
        if (cancelled) return;
        setTasks(loadedTasks);
        setHints({});

        const validTaskIds = new Set(loadedTasks.map((t) => t.id));
        const savedLocal = readLocalDrafts(draftStorageKey);
        const subMap: Record<string, Submission> = {};
        const mergedDrafts: Record<string, string> = {};
        const signedMap: Record<string, string> = {};

        if (profileId && loadedTasks.length > 0) {
          const { data: subRows } = await supabase
            .from('submissions')
            .select('*')
            .eq('student_id', profileId)
            .in(
              'task_id',
              loadedTasks.map((t) => t.id)
            );

          if (cancelled) return;

          for (const sub of (subRows as Submission[]) || []) {
            subMap[sub.task_id] = sub;
            mergedDrafts[sub.task_id] = sub.answer_text || '';
            if (sub.photo_path) {
              const { data: signed } = await supabase.storage
                .from('student-submissions')
                .createSignedUrl(sub.photo_path, 3600);
              if (signed?.signedUrl) signedMap[sub.task_id] = signed.signedUrl;
            }
          }
        }

        const cleanedLocal: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (const [taskId, localVal] of Object.entries(savedLocal)) {
          if (!validTaskIds.has(taskId)) continue;
          const serverVal = subMap[taskId]?.answer_text || '';
          if (localVal !== serverVal) {
            mergedDrafts[taskId] = localVal;
            cleanedLocal[taskId] = localVal;
          }
        }
        writeLocalDrafts(draftStorageKey, cleanedLocal);

        // Определяем статус экзамена СОР / СОЧ (учитывая сдачу на другом устройстве)
        let resolvedSession: ExamSession | null = null;
        if (isStudentExam && loadedTasks.length > 0) {
          const savedSession = readExamSession(examStorageKey);
          const hasAutoChecked = loadedTasks.some(
            (t) => t.type !== 'open' && subMap[t.id]?.status === 'checked'
          );
          const allHaveSubs = loadedTasks.every((t) => Boolean(subMap[t.id]));

          if (hasAutoChecked || allHaveSubs) {
            const now = Date.now();
            resolvedSession = savedSession
              ? { ...savedSession, finishedAt: savedSession.finishedAt || now }
              : { startedAt: now, endsAt: now, finishedAt: now };
            writeExamSession(examStorageKey, resolvedSession);
          } else if (savedSession) {
            resolvedSession = savedSession;
          }
        }

        if (!cancelled) {
          setSubmissions(subMap);
          setDraftAnswers(mergedDrafts);
          setUnsavedDrafts(cleanedLocal);
          setPhotoUrls(signedMap);
          setExamSession(resolvedSession);
          if (resolvedSession && !resolvedSession.finishedAt) {
            setRemainingSec(
              Math.max(0, Math.ceil((resolvedSession.endsAt - Date.now()) / 1000))
            );
          } else {
            setRemainingSec(null);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Homework load error:', err);
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    load();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [topic.id, profileId, draftStorageKey, examStorageKey, isStudentExam]);

  const examStarted = Boolean(examSession);
  const examFinished = Boolean(examSession?.finishedAt);
  const examActive = isStudentExam && tasks.length > 0 && examStarted && !examFinished;
  const examNotStarted = isStudentExam && tasks.length > 0 && !examStarted;
  const examLocked = isStudentExam && examFinished;

  function handleStartExam() {
    playSound('click');
    const now = Date.now();
    const session: ExamSession = {
      startedAt: now,
      endsAt: now + examDurationMin * 60 * 1000,
    };
    writeExamSession(examStorageKey, session);
    setExamSession(session);
    setRemainingSec(examDurationMin * 60);
  }

  const finishExam = useCallback(
    async (autoByTimer: boolean) => {
      if (!profileId || finishLockRef.current) return;

      const currentTasks = tasksRef.current;
      const currentDrafts = draftAnswersRef.current;
      const currentSubs = submissionsRef.current;

      if (!autoByTimer) {
        const answeredCount = currentTasks.filter((t) => {
          const text = (currentDrafts[t.id] || '').trim();
          const hasPhoto = Boolean(currentSubs[t.id]?.photo_path);
          return text.length > 0 || hasPhoto;
        }).length;

        const msg =
          answeredCount < currentTasks.length
            ? `Ты ответил(а) на ${answeredCount} из ${currentTasks.length} заданий. Завершить и сдать работу сейчас? После сдачи изменить ответы будет нельзя.`
            : 'Завершить и сдать СОР / СОЧ на проверку? После сдачи изменить ответы будет нельзя.';

        if (!confirm(msg)) return;
      }

      finishLockRef.current = true;
      setSubmittingExam(true);
      if (autoByTimer) setExamAutoExpired(true);

      const nextSubmissions: Record<string, Submission> = { ...currentSubs };

      try {
        for (const t of currentTasks) {
          const answerText = (currentDrafts[t.id] || '').trim();
          const existingSub = nextSubmissions[t.id];

          // Для открытых задач отправляем, только если есть текст (фото уже сохранено при загрузке)
          if (t.type === 'open') {
            if (!answerText && !existingSub?.photo_path) continue;
            if (existingSub && (existingSub.answer_text || '').trim() === answerText) {
              continue;
            }
          }

          const res = await fetch('/api/homework/check', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              taskId: t.id,
              answerText,
            }),
          });

          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.submission) {
              nextSubmissions[t.id] = data.submission as Submission;
            }
          }
        }

        setSubmissions(nextSubmissions);
        setUnsavedDrafts({});
        writeLocalDrafts(draftStorageKey, {});

        const now = Date.now();
        setExamSession((prev) => {
          const doneSession: ExamSession = prev
            ? { ...prev, finishedAt: now }
            : { startedAt: now, endsAt: now, finishedAt: now };
          writeExamSession(examStorageKey, doneSession);
          return doneSession;
        });
        setRemainingSec(0);

        playSound('celebrate');
        onHomeworkSubmitted?.();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('finishExam error:', err);
        finishLockRef.current = false;
        alert('Не удалось завершить сдачу из-за ошибки сети. Попробуй нажать «Сдать» ещё раз.');
      } finally {
        setSubmittingExam(false);
      }
    },
    [profileId, draftStorageKey, examStorageKey, onHomeworkSubmitted]
  );

  // Живой отсчёт таймера СОР / СОЧ + автоотправка по истечении времени
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!examActive || !examSession) return;

    const tick = () => {
      const left = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((examSession.endsAt - Date.now()) / 1000));
      setRemainingSec(left);
      if (left <= 0) {
        void finishExam(true);
      }
    };

    tick();
    const id = setInterval(tick, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [examActive, examSession, finishExam]);

  function handleDraftChange(taskId: string, nextValue: string) {
    if (examLocked) return;
    setDraftAnswers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [taskId]: nextValue }));
    setUnsavedDrafts((prev) => {
      const serverVal = submissions[taskId]?.answer_text || '';
      const next = { ...prev };
      if (nextValue === serverVal) {
        delete next[taskId];
      } else {
        next[taskId] = nextValue;
      }
      writeLocalDrafts(draftStorageKey, next);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function clearLocalDraft(taskId: string) {
    setUnsavedDrafts((prev) => {
      if (!(taskId in prev)) return prev;
      const next = { ...prev };
      delete next[taskId];
      writeLocalDrafts(draftStorageKey, next);
      return next;
    });
  }

  async function submitAnswer(taskId: string, answerText: string, photoPath?: string | null) {
    if (!profile) {
      alert('Войди в аккаунт, чтобы решать и отправлять домашние задания.');
      return;
    }
    if (examLocked) return;

    setBusyTaskId(taskId);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/homework/check', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ taskId, answerText, photoPath }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        alert(data.error || 'Ошибка проверки ответа');
        return;
      }

      const sub = data.submission as Submission;
      if (sub.status === 'checked') {
        playSound(sub.is_correct ? 'success' : 'error');
      } else {
        playSound('success');
      }

      const nextSubmissions = { ...submissions, [taskId]: sub };
      setSubmissions(nextSubmissions);
      setDraftAnswers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [taskId]: sub.answer_text || '' }));
      clearLocalDraft(taskId);

      // В обычном ДЗ зажигаем звезду, когда все задания урока выполнены
      if (!isStudentExam) {
        const allCompleted =
          tasks.length > 0 &&
          tasks.every((t) => {
            const s = nextSubmissions[t.id];
            if (!s) return false;
            return t.type === 'open' ? true : Boolean(s.is_correct);
          });
        if (allCompleted) {
          onHomeworkSubmitted?.();
        }
      }

      if (!isStudentExam && data.hint) {
        setHints((prev) => ({ ...prev, [taskId]: data.hint }));
      } else {
        setHints((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          delete next[taskId];
          return next;
        });
      }
    } finally {
      setBusyTaskId(null);
    }
  }

  async function handlePhotoUpload(taskId: string, file: File) {
    if (!profile) {
      alert('Войди в аккаунт, чтобы прикрепить фото тетради.');
      return;
    }
    if (examLocked) return;

    setBusyTaskId(taskId);
    const preparedFile = await compressImageFile(file);

    if (preparedFile.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      alert('Фото всё ещё слишком большое (макс. 5 МБ).');
      setBusyTaskId(null);
      return;
    }

    const ext = preparedFile.name.split('.').pop() || 'jpg';
    const path = `${profile.id}/${taskId}_${Date.now()}.${ext}`;

    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
      .from('student-submissions')
      .upload(path, preparedFile);

    if (upErr) {
      alert('Ошибка загрузки фото: ' + upErr.message);
      setBusyTaskId(null);
      return;
    }

    const { data: signed } = await supabase.storage
      .from('student-submissions')
      .createSignedUrl(path, 3600);
    if (signed?.signedUrl) {
      setPhotoUrls((prev) => ({ ...prev, [taskId]: signed.signedUrl }));
    }

    await submitAnswer(taskId, draftAnswers[taskId] || '', path);
  }

  async function handleCreateTask(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!questionMd.trim()) return;

    const options =
      hwType === 'test'
        ? optionsText
            .split(';')
            .map((s) => s.trim())
            .filter(Boolean)
        : [];

    if (hwType === 'test' && options.length < 2) {
      alert('Для теста укажите минимум 2 варианта ответа через точку с запятой (;)');
      return;
    }
    if (hwType !== 'open' && !correctAnswer.trim()) {
      alert('Укажите правильный ответ для автопроверки.');
      return;
    }

    setCreating(true);
    const nextOrder = tasks.length
      ? Math.max(...tasks.map((t) => t.order_index)) + 1
      : 1;

    const { data: created, error } = await supabase
      .from('homework_tasks')
      .insert({
        topic_id: topic.id,
        order_index: nextOrder,
        type: hwType,
        difficulty,
        question_md: questionMd.trim(),
        options,
        points: Math.max(1, points),
      })
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error || !created) {
      alert('Ошибка создания задания: ' + error?.message);
      setCreating(false);
      return;
    }

    if (hwType !== 'open') {
      const { error: keyErr } = await supabase.from('homework_answer_keys').insert({
        task_id: created.id,
        correct_answer: correctAnswer.trim(),
        solution_hint: solutionHint.trim(),
      });
      if (keyErr) {
        alert('Задание создано, но ключ ответа не сохранился: ' + keyErr.message);
      }
    }

    setTasks((prev) => [...prev, created as HomeworkTask]);
    setQuestionMd('');
    setOptionsText('');
    setCorrectAnswer('');
    setSolutionHint('');
    setCreating(false);
  }

  async function handleGenerateAi() {
    setAiLoading(true);
    setAiError('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/teacher/generate-homework', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          topicId: topic.id,
          count: aiCount,
          extraWish: aiWish,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setAiError(data.error || 'Ошибка генерации');
        return;
      }
      setAiDrafts(data.tasks || []);
    } catch {
      setAiError('Сетевая ошибка при обращении к ИИ.');
    } finally {
      setAiLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function updateDraft(index: number, patch: Partial<DraftGenTask>) {
    setAiDrafts((prev) =>
      prev.map((item, idx) => (idx === index ? { ...item, ...patch } : item))
    );
  }

  async function handleSaveAiDrafts() {
    if (aiDrafts.length === 0) return;
    setSavingDrafts(true);
    let nextOrder = tasks.length
      ? Math.max(...tasks.map((t) => t.order_index)) + 1
      : 1;
    const inserted: HomeworkTask[] = [];

    for (const draft of aiDrafts) {
      if (!draft.question_md.trim()) continue;
      const { data: created, error } = await supabase
        .from('homework_tasks')
        .insert({
          topic_id: topic.id,
          order_index: nextOrder++,
          type: draft.type,
          difficulty: draft.difficulty,
          question_md: draft.question_md.trim(),
          options: draft.type === 'test' ? draft.options : [],
          points: Math.max(1, draft.points),
        })
        .select()
        .single();

      if (!error && created) {
        inserted.push(created as HomeworkTask);
        if (draft.type !== 'open') {
          await supabase.from('homework_answer_keys').insert({
            task_id: created.id,
            correct_answer: draft.correct_answer.trim(),
            solution_hint: draft.solution_hint.trim(),
          });
        }
      }
    }

    if (inserted.length > 0) {
      setTasks((prev) => [...prev, ...inserted]);
      setAiDrafts([]);
      setAiWish('');
    }
    setSavingDrafts(false);
  }

  async function handleDeleteTask(taskId: string) {
    if (!confirm('Удалить это домашнее задание?')) return;
    const { error } = await supabase.from('homework_tasks').delete().eq('id', taskId);
    if (!error) {
      setTasks((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== taskId));
      clearLocalDraft(taskId);
    }
  }

  const maxPoints = tasks.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.points, 0);
  const earnedPoints = tasks.reduce(
    (sum, t) => sum + (submissions[t.id]?.score || 0),
    0
  );
  const answeredInExamCount = tasks.filter((t) => {
    const txt = (draftAnswers[t.id] || '').trim();
    return txt.length > 0 || Boolean(submissions[t.id]?.photo_path);
  }).length;

  const isLowTime = remainingSec !== null && remainingSec <= 120;
  const isWarnTime = remainingSec !== null && remainingSec <= 300 && !isLowTime;

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
        Загрузка домашних заданий...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Инфо-баннер для учителя на темах СОР/СОЧ */}
      {topic.is_assessment && isTeacher && (
        <div className="rounded-3xl bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 px-5 py-3.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-amber-950 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div>
            🛡️ <strong>Защита СОР / СОЧ активна для учеников:</strong> индивидуальное
            перемешивание заданий и вариантов, таймер <strong>{examDurationMin} мин</strong>,
            единая сдача без подбора и отключённый Сократ.
          </div>
          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-white border border-amber-200 text-xs font-extrabold text-amber-800">
            Просмотр учителя
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Липкая панель таймера и сдачи во время активного СОР / СОЧ */}
      {examActive && (
        <div
          className={`sticky top-[72px] z-20 rounded-2xl border-2 px-4 py-3 shadow-md flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 backdrop-blur-md ${
            isLowTime
              ? 'bg-rose-50/95 border-rose-400 text-rose-900'
              : isWarnTime
                ? 'bg-amber-50/95 border-amber-400 text-amber-950'
                : 'bg-white/95 border-indigo-300 text-slate-900'
          }`}
        >
