          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 text-xs font-extrabold">
              🛡️ СОР / СОЧ · {examVariant}
            </span>
            <span
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl font-mono text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold tabular-nums ${
                isLowTime
                  ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white animate-pulse'
                  : isWarnTime
                    ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
                    : 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
              }`}
            >
              ⏱️ {formatCountdown(remainingSec ?? 0)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold opacity-80 tabular-nums">
              Отвечено: {answeredInExamCount} / {tasks.length}
            </span>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={submittingExam}
            onClick={() => void finishExam(false)}
            className="btn-tactile px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold"
          >
            {submittingExam ? 'Отправляем...' : '📤 Сдать работу'}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Итоговый баннер после завершения СОР / СОЧ */}
      {examLocked && (
        <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-50 to-teal-50 border-2 border-emerald-300 p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-pop">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-emerald-950">
              {examAutoExpired
                ? '⏰ Время вышло — ответы автоматически сохранены!'
                : '🏁 Работа СОР / СОЧ успешно сдана!'}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-emerald-800">
              {examVariant} · Изменение ответов закрыто. Если есть открытые задачи,
              учитель проверит их и выставит дополнительные баллы.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 text-sm font-extrabold tabular-nums">
            Итог: {earnedPoints} / {maxPoints} б.
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Обычная шапка ДЗ (не показываем во время активного экзамена) */}
      {!examActive && !examNotStarted && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              {topic.is_assessment ? 'Задания СОР / СОЧ' : 'Домашние задания к уроку'}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Тесты и краткие ответы проверяются мгновенно; черновики сохраняются автоматически.
            </p>
          </div>
          {tasks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold tabular-nums">
              Набрано: {earnedPoints} / {maxPoints} б.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Экран старта СОР / СОЧ для ученика */}
      {examNotStarted ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border-2 border-amber-300 shadow-sm p-6 sm:p-8 text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-5 animate-pop">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl">
            🛡️
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 text-xs font-extrabold">
              <span>🏆 СОР / СОЧ</span>
              <span>·</span>
              <span>{examVariant}</span>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">
              Готов(а) начать проверочную работу?
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 max-w-lg mx-auto leading-relaxed">
              После нажатия кнопки запустится таймер. Ответы сохраняются автоматически,
              а итоговый результат появится сразу после сдачи работы.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 max-w-md mx-auto">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
              <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-indigo-600 tabular-nums">
                {tasks.length}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">заданий</div>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
              <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-amber-600 tabular-nums">
                {examDurationMin} мин
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">на работу</div>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
              <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-emerald-600 tabular-nums">
                {maxPoints} б.
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">максимум</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleStartExam}
              className="btn-tactile px-7 py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 text-white text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold shadow-xs"
            >
              🚀 Начать СОР / СОЧ
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : tasks.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 p-8 text-center space-y-2">
          <div className="text-3xl">📝</div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
            Для этой темы домашнее задание ещё не назначено
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            {canEdit
              ? 'Нажми «Сгенерировать через ИИ» ниже или добавь задание вручную.'
              : 'Загляни во вкладку «2. Потренироваться», чтобы попрактиковаться!'}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {displayTasks.map((hw, idx) => {
            const sub = submissions[hw.id];
            const diff = DIFF_META[hw.difficulty] || DIFF_META.standard;
            const busy = busyTaskId === hw.id || submittingExam;
            const hasLocalDraft = hw.id in unsavedDrafts;
            const typeLabel =
              hw.type === 'test'
                ? 'Тест'
                : hw.type === 'input'
                  ? 'Краткий ответ'
                  : 'Развёрнутое решение';

            return (
              <div
                key={hw.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-5 space-y-3.5"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">
                      Задание №{idx + 1}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold border ${diff.cls}`}
                    >
                      {diff.label}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
                      {typeLabel} · {hw.points} б.
                    </span>
                    {examActive && (draftAnswers[hw.id] || '').trim() && (
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 text-[10px] font-bold">
                        ✓ Ответ записан
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {!examActive && hasLocalDraft && (
                      <span
                        className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-sky-50 border border-sky-200 text-sky-700 text-[10px] font-bold"
                        title="Введённый текст сохранён в браузере и не пропадёт при обновлении страницы"
                      >
                        💾 Черновик сохранён
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {canEdit && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteTask(hw.id)}
                      className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-700"
                    >
                      Удалить
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <MathMarkdown content={hw.question_md} />

                {/* Тест */}
                {hw.type === 'test' && (
                  <div className="space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      {hw.options.map((opt) => {
                        const chosenInExam = examActive && draftAnswers[hw.id] === opt;
                        const chosenSubmitted = !examActive && sub?.answer_text === opt;

                        let cls =
                          'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 hover:border-indigo-300 text-slate-700';
                        if (chosenInExam) {
                          cls =
                            'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-900 ring-2 ring-indigo-200';
                        } else if (chosenSubmitted && sub?.status === 'checked') {
                          cls = sub.is_correct
                            ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900'
                            : 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-50 text-rose-900';
                        }

                        return (
                          <button
                            key={opt}
                            type="button"
                            disabled={busy || examLocked}
                            onClick={() => {
                              if (examActive) {
                                playSound('click');
                                handleDraftChange(hw.id, opt);
                              } else {
                                void submitAnswer(hw.id, opt);
                              }
                            }}
                            className={`text-left px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl border-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold transition disabled:cursor-default ${cls}`}
                          >
                            <MathMarkdown content={opt} inline />
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>

                    {!examActive && sub?.status === 'checked' && (
                      <div
                        className={`text-xs font-bold ${
                          sub.is_correct ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {sub.is_correct
                          ? `✓ Верно! Начислено ${sub.score} б.`
                          : examLocked
                            ? '✗ Ответ неверный (0 б.).'
                            : '✗ Неверно. Попробуй другой вариант!'}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Краткий ответ с умной мат-клавиатурой и автосохранением */}
                {hw.type === 'input' && (
                  <div className="space-y-2.5">
                    <HomeworkShortInput
                      value={draftAnswers[hw.id] ?? ''}
                      busy={busy}
                      examMode={examActive || examLocked}
                      disabled={examLocked}
                      onChange={(next) => handleDraftChange(hw.id, next)}
                      onSubmit={() => submitAnswer(hw.id, draftAnswers[hw.id] || '')}
                    />

                    {!examActive && sub?.status === 'checked' && (
                      <div
                        className={`text-xs font-bold ${
                          sub.is_correct ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {sub.is_correct
                          ? `✓ Верно! Начислено ${sub.score} б.`
                          : examLocked
                            ? '✗ Ответ неверный (0 б.).'
                            : '✗ Ответ не совпадает. Перепроверь вычисления и попробуй ещё раз.'}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {!isStudentExam && hints[hw.id] && (
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-xs text-amber-900">
                    💡 <strong>Подсказка:</strong> {hints[hw.id]}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Развёрнутое решение с автосохранением текста */}
                {hw.type === 'open' && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      disabled={examLocked}
                      value={draftAnswers[hw.id] ?? ''}
                      onChange={(e) => handleDraftChange(hw.id, e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Напиши ход решения здесь (или прикрепи фото тетради)..."
                      className="w-full p-3.5 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-60"
                    />

                    {!examLocked && (
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <label className="cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition">
                          📷 {busy ? 'Сжимаем и загружаем...' : 'Прикрепить фото тетради'}
                          <input
                            type="file"
                            accept="image/*"
                            disabled={busy}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                              if (f) void handlePhotoUpload(hw.id, f);
                              e.target.value = '';
                            }}
                            className="hidden"
                          />
                        </label>

                        {!examActive && (
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={busy}
                            onClick={() => submitAnswer(hw.id, draftAnswers[hw.id] || '')}
                            className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition"
                          >
                            {sub ? 'Обновить решение' : 'Отправить учителю'}
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {photoUrls[hw.id] && (
                      <div className="pt-1">
                        <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1">
                          Прикреплённое фото тетради:
                        </p>
                        <a href={photoUrls[hw.id]} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                          <img
                            src={photoUrls[hw.id]}
                            alt="Фото решения"
                            className="max-h-56 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs"
                          />
                        </a>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {sub?.status === 'pending_review' && (
                      <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-200 text-xs font-semibold text-sky-800">
                        ⏳ Решение отправлено и ожидает проверки учителя.
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {sub?.status === 'graded' && (
                      <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-xs text-emerald-900 space-y-1">
                        <div className="font-bold">
                          ✓ Проверено учителем: {sub.score} / {hw.points} б.
                        </div>
                        {sub.teacher_comment && (
                          <div>
                            <strong>Комментарий:</strong> {sub.teacher_comment}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}

          {/* Нижняя кнопка сдачи в конце СОР / СОЧ */}
          {examActive && (
            <div className="rounded-3xl bg-white border-2 border-emerald-300 p-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
                Проверил(а) все ответы? Отвечено {answeredInExamCount} из {tasks.length}.
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={submittingExam}
                onClick={() => void finishExam(false)}
                className="btn-tactile px-6 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-sm font-extrabold"
              >
                {submittingExam ? 'Отправляем...' : '📤 Завершить и сдать СОР / СОЧ'}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ИИ-генератор ДЗ (только для учителя) */}
      {canEdit && (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-50 via-white to-indigo-50 rounded-3xl border-2 border-violet-200 shadow-xs p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-violet-950">
                ✨ ИИ-генератор ДЗ по теме и ЦО
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                Gemini составит разноуровневый комплект (База / Стандарт / Профи) с ключами. Ты проверишь и сохранишь.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-violet-200">
              {([3, 5] as const).map((cnt) => (
                <button
                  key={cnt}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAiCount(cnt)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
                    aiCount === cnt ? 'bg-violet-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  {cnt} задания
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={aiWish}
              onChange={(e) => setAiWish(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Пожелание (необязательно, напр.: добавь текстовую задачу про покупки в тенге)"
              className="flex-1 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-white border border-violet-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={aiLoading}
              onClick={handleGenerateAi}
              className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-700 border-violet-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold shadow-xs transition shrink-0"
            >
              {aiLoading ? 'Генерация...' : `✨ Сгенерировать (${aiCount})`}
            </button>
          </div>

          {aiError && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-700">
              {aiError}
            </div>
          )}

          {aiDrafts.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-violet-200/70">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-violet-900">
                  Проверь черновики перед добавлением в урок ({aiDrafts.length}):
                </span>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setAiDrafts([])}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600"
                  >
                    Отменить
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={savingDrafts}
                    onClick={handleSaveAiDrafts}
                    className="btn-tactile px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs font-bold transition"
                  >
                    {savingDrafts ? 'Сохранение...' : `💾 Сохранить все (${aiDrafts.length})`}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {aiDrafts.map((draft, i) => (
                <div
                  key={i}
                  className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-violet-200 p-4 space-y-2.5"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold">
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-violet-100 text-violet-800">
                        #{i + 1} · {draft.type === 'test' ? 'Тест' : draft.type === 'input' ? 'Ответ' : 'Открытая'}
                      </span>
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">
                        {DIFF_META[draft.difficulty].label} · {draft.points} б.
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        setAiDrafts((prev) => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))
                      }
                      className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-500 hover:underline"
                    >
                      Убрать
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={draft.question_md}
                    onChange={(e) => updateDraft(i, { question_md: e.target.value })}
                    className="w-full p-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
                  />

                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50/70 border border-slate-100">
                    <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
                      Как увидит ученик:
                    </div>
                    <MathMarkdown content={draft.question_md} />
                  </div>

                  {draft.type === 'test' && (
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={draft.options.join('; ')}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        updateDraft(i, {
                          options: e.target.value
                            .split(';')
                            .map((s) => s.trim())
                            .filter(Boolean),
                        })
                      }
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
                      placeholder="Варианты через ;"
                    />
                  )}

                  {draft.type !== 'open' && (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={draft.correct_answer}
                        onChange={(e) => updateDraft(i, { correct_answer: e.target.value })}
                        className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800 bg-emerald-50/50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl"
                        placeholder="Правильный ответ"
                      />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={draft.solution_hint}
                        onChange={(e) => updateDraft(i, { solution_hint: e.target.value })}
                        className="px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-amber-50/40 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl"
                        placeholder="Подсказка при ошибке"
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Ручной конструктор нового задания */}
      {canEdit && (
        <form
          onSubmit={handleCreateTask}
          className="bg-white rounded-3xl border-2 border-indigo-200 shadow-xs p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h4 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-indigo-900">
              🛠️ Добавить задание вручную
            </h4>
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-600">
              Поддерживает формулы $LaTeX$
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            <select
              value={hwType}
              onChange={(e) => setHwType(e.target.value as typeof hwType)}
              className="sm:col-span-2 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option value="test">Тест с выбором варианта (автопроверка)</option>
              <option value="input">Краткий ответ / число (автопроверка)</option>
              <option value="open">Развёрнутая задача (текст + фото тетради)</option>
            </select>

            <select
              value={difficulty}
              onChange={(e) => setDifficulty(e.target.value as typeof difficulty)}
              className="px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option value="base">Уровень: База</option>
              <option value="standard">Уровень: Стандарт</option>
              <option value="pro">Уровень: Профи</option>
            </select>

            <input
              type="number"
              min={1}
              max={20}
              value={points}
              onChange={(e) => setPoints(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder="Баллы"
              className="px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>

          <textarea
            rows={2}
            required
            value={questionMd}
            onChange={(e) => setQuestionMd(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Условие задачи (можно с формулами, например: Найдите $\frac{3}{4}$ от 60)"
            className="w-full p-3.5 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
          />

          {hwType === 'test' && (
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={optionsText}
              onChange={(e) => setOptionsText(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Варианты через точку с запятой (например: 15; 30; 45; 60)"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
          )}

          {hwType !== 'open' && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={correctAnswer}
                onChange={(e) => setCorrectAnswer(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Правильный ответ (хранится закрыто на сервере)"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={solutionHint}
                onChange={(e) => setSolutionHint(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Подсказка при ошибке (необязательно)"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={creating}
            className="btn-tactile px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold shadow-xs transition"
          >
            {creating ? 'Сохраняем...' : '+ Добавить домашнее задание'}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/InteractiveTab.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { playSound } from '@/lib/audio/sound';
import PercentGridSim from '@/components/simulators/PercentGridSim';
import DiagramsSim from '@/components/simulators/DiagramsSim';
import CoordRaySim from '@/components/simulators/CoordRaySim';
import DivisibilitySim from '@/components/simulators/DivisibilitySim';
import FractionsSim from '@/components/simulators/FractionsSim';
import ProtractorSim from '@/components/simulators/ProtractorSim';
import SetsVennSim from '@/components/simulators/SetsVennSim';
import TaskGeneratorSim from '@/components/simulators/TaskGeneratorSim';
import type { Material, Profile, Topic } from '@/types/db';

type SimId =
  | 'coord_ray'
  | 'divisibility'
  | 'fractions_visual'
  | 'angles'
  | 'sets'
  | 'percent_grid'
  | 'diagrams'
  | 'generator';

const SIMULATORS: { id: SimId; name: string; icon: string }[] = [
  { id: 'coord_ray', name: 'Координатный луч', icon: '📏' },
  { id: 'divisibility', name: 'Блиц: Делимость', icon: '⚡' },
  { id: 'fractions_visual', name: 'Пицца дробей', icon: '🍕' },
  { id: 'angles', name: 'Транспортир', icon: '📐' },
  { id: 'sets', name: 'Эйлер–Венн', icon: '⭕' },
  { id: 'percent_grid', name: 'Проценты 10×10', icon: '💯' },
  { id: 'diagrams', name: 'Диаграммы', icon: '📊' },
  { id: 'generator', name: 'Генератор задач', icon: '🎲' },
];

function resolveDefaultSim(simType: string): SimId {
  const direct: SimId[] = [
    'coord_ray',
    'divisibility',
    'fractions_visual',
    'angles',
    'sets',
    'percent_grid',
    'diagrams',
  ];
  return direct.includes(simType as SimId) ? (simType as SimId) : 'generator';
}

interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  materials: Material[];
  canEdit: boolean;
  profile: Profile | null;
  isTopicCompleted: boolean;
  onMaterialsChanged: (next: Material[]) => void;
  onProblemSolved?: (totalSolved: number) => void;
}

export default function InteractiveTab({
  topic,
  materials,
  canEdit,
  profile,
  isTopicCompleted,
  onMaterialsChanged,
  onProblemSolved,
}: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [activeSim, setActiveSim] = useState<SimId>(() =>
    resolveDefaultSim(topic.sim_type)
  );
  const [correctCount, setCorrectCount] = useState(0);
  const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0);

  const [embedTitle, setEmbedTitle] = useState('');
  const [embedInput, setEmbedInput] = useState('');

  // При смене темы подбираем профильный симулятор и подгружаем счётчик ученика из БД
  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveSim(resolveDefaultSim(topic.sim_type));
    if (!profile) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    supabase
      .from('topic_progress')
      .select('sim_correct_count, sim_streak')
      .eq('student_id', profile.id)
      .eq('topic_id', topic.id)
      .maybeSingle()
      .then(({ data }) => {
        if (!cancelled && data) {
          setCorrectCount(data.sim_correct_count || 0);
          setStreak(data.sim_streak || 0);
        } else if (!cancelled) {
          setCorrectCount(0);
          setStreak(0);
        }
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [topic.id, topic.sim_type, profile?.id]);

  async function handleSimResult(isCorrect: boolean) {
    const nextCorrect = isCorrect ? correctCount + 1 : correctCount;
    const nextStreak = isCorrect ? streak + 1 : 0;
    setCorrectCount(nextCorrect);
    setStreak(nextStreak);

    if (isCorrect) {
      playSound(nextStreak > 0 && nextStreak % 5 === 0 ? 'celebrate' : 'success');
    } else {
      playSound('error');
    }

    if (!profile) return;
    if (isCorrect) {
      onProblemSolved?.(nextCorrect);
    }

    await supabase.from('topic_progress').upsert({
      student_id: profile.id,
      topic_id: topic.id,
      completed: isTopicCompleted,
      sim_correct_count: nextCorrect,
      sim_streak: nextStreak,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  async function handleAddEmbed(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!embedTitle.trim() || !embedInput.trim()) return;
    const match = embedInput.match(/src\s*=\s*["']([^"']+)["']/i);
    const src = match ? match[1] : embedInput.trim();
    if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(src)) {
      alert('Укажите корректную ссылку https://... или код <iframe src="...">');
      return;
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('materials')
      .insert({
        topic_id: topic.id,
        kind: 'iframe_game',
        title: embedTitle.trim(),
        url: src,
      })
      .select()
      .single();

    if (!error && data) {
      onMaterialsChanged([...materials, data as Material]);
      setEmbedTitle('');
      setEmbedInput('');
    }
  }

  async function handleDeleteEmbed(id: string) {
    await supabase.from('materials').delete().eq('id', id);
    onMaterialsChanged(materials.filter((m) => m.id !== id));
  }

  const embeds = materials.filter((m) => m.kind === 'iframe_game');

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Встроенные симуляторы */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              Интерактивная лаборатория
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Тренажёр подобран под текущую тему, но можно открыть любую песочницу.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
            <span className="text-emerald-800">
              🏆 Решено: <strong>{correctCount}</strong>
            </span>
            <span className="text-emerald-300">|</span>
            <span className="text-amber-700">
              🔥 Серия: <strong>{streak}</strong>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {SIMULATORS.map((s) => (
            <button
              key={s.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveSim(s.id)}
              className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                activeSim === s.id
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600 shadow-xs'
                  : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{s.icon}</span> {s.name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 sm:p-6">
          {activeSim === 'percent_grid' && <PercentGridSim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'diagrams' && <DiagramsSim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'coord_ray' && <CoordRaySim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'divisibility' && <DivisibilitySim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'fractions_visual' && <FractionsSim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'angles' && <ProtractorSim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'sets' && <SetsVennSim onResult={handleSimResult} />}
          {activeSim === 'generator' && (
            <TaskGeneratorSim initialSimType={topic.sim_type} onResult={handleSimResult} />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Внешние игры */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
            🕹️ Внешние игры (Wordwall, GeoGebra, LearningApps, Desmos)
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            Учитель может встроить готовую интерактивную игру по ссылке или iframe-коду.
          </p>
        </div>

        {embeds.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-6 text-center rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-dashed border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Внешние игры к этой теме пока не добавлены.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {embeds.map((em) => (
              <div
                key={em.id}
                className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden bg-white"
              >
                <div className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-bold">
                  <span>{em.title}</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <a
                      href={em.url || '#'}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="text-indigo-600 hover:underline"
                    >
                      На весь экран ↗
                    </a>
                    {canEdit && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteEmbed(em.id)}
                        className="text-rose-600 hover:underline"
                      >
                        Удалить
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="aspect-video bg-slate-100">
                  <iframe
                    src={em.url || ''}
                    className="w-full h-full border-0"
                    allowFullScreen
                    loading="lazy"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {canEdit && (
          <form
            onSubmit={handleAddEmbed}
            className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5"
          >
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={embedTitle}
              onChange={(e) => setEmbedTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Название игры (напр. Wordwall)"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={embedInput}
              onChange={(e) => setEmbedInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="URL или код <iframe src='...'>"
              className="px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="py-2 px-4 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition"
            >
              + Встроить игру
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/LessonSlidesPlayer.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import MathMarkdown from './MathMarkdown';
import SlideVisualBox from './SlideVisualBox';
import { playSound } from '@/lib/audio/sound';
import type { LessonSlide, SlideType, Topic } from '@/types/db';

interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  boardMode?: boolean;
  isTeacherView?: boolean;
  onSlidesCompleted?: () => void;
  onGoToPractice?: () => void;
}

const SLIDE_META: Record<
  SlideType,
  { icon: string; badge: string; badgeCls: string; accentBorder: string }
> = {
  intro: {
    icon: '🚀',
    badge: 'Зачем это нужно',
    badgeCls: 'bg-sky-100 text-sky-800',
    accentBorder: 'border-sky-200',
  },
  concept: {
    icon: '💡',
    badge: 'Главное правило',
    badgeCls: 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800',
    accentBorder: 'border-indigo-200',
  },
  example: {
    icon: '👀',
    badge: 'Пошаговый разбор',
    badgeCls: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800',
    accentBorder: 'border-emerald-200',
  },
  trap: {
    icon: '⚠️',
    badge: 'Осторожно, ловушка!',
    badgeCls: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900',
    accentBorder: 'border-amber-300',
  },
  quiz: {
    icon: '🎯',
    badge: 'Мини-проверка',
    badgeCls: 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-800',
    accentBorder: 'border-violet-300',
  },
  summary: {
    icon: '⭐',
    badge: 'Шпаргалка урока',
    badgeCls: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800',
    accentBorder: 'border-emerald-300',
  },
};

function buildFallbackSlides(topic: Topic): LessonSlide[] {
  const md = (topic.theory_md || '').trim();
  if (!md) {
    return [
      {
        id: 'empty-1',
        type: 'intro',
        title: topic.title,
        body_md:
          topic.value_example ||
          'Конспект и презентация для этого урока скоро появятся. Загляни во вкладку «2. Потренироваться»!',
      },
    ];
  }

  const chunks = md
    .split(/\n(?=###\s+)/)
    .map((c) => c.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

  return chunks.map((chunk, idx) => {
    const lines = chunk.split('\n');
    let title = idx === 0 ? topic.title : `Шаг ${idx + 1}`;
    let body = chunk;

    if (lines[0]?.startsWith('###')) {
      title = lines[0].replace(/^###\s*/, '').trim();
      body = lines.slice(1).join('\n').trim() || title;
    }

    const lower = title.toLowerCase();
    let type: SlideType = idx === 0 ? 'intro' : 'concept';
    if (lower.includes('пример') || lower.includes('разбор')) type = 'example';
    if (
      lower.includes('ошибк') ||
      lower.includes('ловушк') ||
      lower.includes('важно')
    ) {
      type = 'trap';
    }
    if (idx === chunks.length - 1 && chunks.length > 2) type = 'summary';

    return {
      id: `auto-${idx}`,
      type,
      title,
      body_md: body,
    };
  });
}

export default function LessonSlidesPlayer({
  topic,
  boardMode = false,
  isTeacherView = false,
  onSlidesCompleted,
  onGoToPractice,
}: Props) {
  const hasRichSlides = Array.isArray(topic.slides) && topic.slides.length > 0;
  const slides = useMemo(
    () =>
      hasRichSlides ? (topic.slides as LessonSlide[]) : buildFallbackSlides(topic),
    [hasRichSlides, topic]
  );

  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'slides' | 'full'>('slides');
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [revealedSteps, setRevealedSteps] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [quizAnswers, setQuizAnswers] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  const [completedDeck, setCompletedDeck] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIndex(0);
    setRevealedSteps({});
    setQuizAnswers({});
    setCompletedDeck(false);
  }, [topic.id, slides.length]);

  const safeIndex = Math.min(index, Math.max(0, slides.length - 1));
  const slide = slides[safeIndex];
  const meta = SLIDE_META[slide?.type || 'concept'] || SLIDE_META.concept;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (viewMode !== 'slides') return;
    function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || tag === 'SELECT') return;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        setIndex((i) => Math.min(slides.length - 1, i + 1));
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        setIndex((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1));
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [viewMode, slides.length]);

  function goPrev() {
    if (safeIndex <= 0) return;
    playSound('click');
    setIndex(safeIndex - 1);
  }

  function goNext() {
    if (safeIndex >= slides.length - 1) {
      playSound('celebrate');
      setCompletedDeck(true);
      onSlidesCompleted?.();
      return;
    }
    playSound('click');
    setIndex(safeIndex + 1);
  }

  function handleSelectQuiz(slideId: string, optionIdx: number, correctIdx: number) {
    setQuizAnswers((prev) => ({ ...prev, [slideId]: optionIdx }));
    if (optionIdx === correctIdx) {
      playSound('success');
    } else {
      playSound('error');
    }
  }

  if (!slide) return null;

  const progressPct = Math.round(((safeIndex + 1) / slides.length) * 100);
  const steps = slide.steps || [];
  const shownStepsCount = revealedSteps[slide.id] ?? 1;
  const chosenQuizOpt = quizAnswers[slide.id];
  const quizCorrectIdx = slide.quiz_correct_index ?? 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                playSound('click');
                setViewMode('slides');
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                viewMode === 'slides'
                  ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600'
              }`}
            >
              🎬 Презентация ({slides.length})
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                playSound('click');
                setViewMode('full');
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                viewMode === 'full'
                  ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600'
              }`}
            >
              📄 Весь конспект
            </button>
          </div>

          {!hasRichSlides && isTeacherView && (
            <span className="hidden sm:inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-violet-50 border border-violet-200 text-[11px] font-bold text-violet-700">
              Автослайды · Нажми «✨ Собрать презентацию» ниже
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {viewMode === 'slides' && (
          <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
            Слайд {safeIndex + 1} из {slides.length}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {viewMode === 'slides' ? (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {slides.map((s, i) => {
              const isCurrent = i === safeIndex;
              const isPast = i < safeIndex;
              const isQuiz = s.type === 'quiz';
              return (
                <button
                  key={s.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    playSound('click');
                    setIndex(i);
                  }}
                  title={`Слайд ${i + 1}: ${s.title}`}
                  className={`h-2.5 flex-1 rounded-full transition-all ${
                    isCurrent
                      ? isQuiz
                        ? 'bg-violet-600 scale-y-125'
                        : 'bg-indigo-600 scale-y-125'
                      : isPast
                        ? 'bg-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-500'
                        : 'bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300'
                  }`}
                />
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div
            key={slide.id}
            className={`rounded-3xl bg-white border-2 ${meta.accentBorder} p-5 sm:p-7 shadow-xs space-y-5 animate-pop min-h-[340px] flex flex-col justify-between`}
          >
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <span
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-extrabold ${meta.badgeCls}`}
                >
                  <span>{meta.icon}</span>
                  <span>{meta.badge}</span>
                </span>
                <h3
                  className={`font-extrabold text-slate-900 leading-tight ${
                    boardMode ? 'text-2xl sm:text-3xl' : 'text-xl sm:text-2xl'
                  }`}
                >
                  {slide.title}
                </h3>
              </div>

              {slide.body_md && (
                <MathMarkdown content={slide.body_md} large={boardMode} />
              )}

              {slide.visual && (
                <SlideVisualBox visual={slide.visual} large={boardMode} />
              )}

              {steps.length > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
                  {steps.slice(0, shownStepsCount).map((stepText, stepIdx) => (
                    <div
                      key={stepIdx}
                      className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-200 animate-pop"
                    >
                      <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                        {stepIdx + 1}
                      </span>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <MathMarkdown content={stepText} large={boardMode} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}

                  {shownStepsCount < steps.length && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          playSound('click');
                          setRevealedSteps((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [slide.id]: shownStepsCount + 1,
                          }));
                        }}
                        className="btn-tactile px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold"
                      >
                        👀 Показать шаг {shownStepsCount + 1} из {steps.length}
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          playSound('click');
                          setRevealedSteps((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [slide.id]: steps.length,
                          }));
                        }}
                        className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold"
                      >
                        Открыть все шаги
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {(slide.wrong_md || slide.right_md) && (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1">
                  {slide.wrong_md && (
                    <div className="rounded-2xl bg-rose-50 border-2 border-rose-200 p-4 space-y-1.5">
                      <div className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-rose-700">
                        ❌ Частая ошибка
                      </div>
                      <MathMarkdown content={slide.wrong_md} large={boardMode} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {slide.right_md && (
                    <div className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 border-2 border-emerald-200 p-4 space-y-1.5">
                      <div className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-emerald-700">
                        ✅ Как правильно
                      </div>
                      <MathMarkdown content={slide.right_md} large={boardMode} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {slide.type === 'quiz' &&
                slide.quiz_options &&
                slide.quiz_options.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                      {slide.quiz_options.map((opt, optIdx) => {
                        const isPicked = chosenQuizOpt === optIdx;
                        const isRight = optIdx === quizCorrectIdx;
                        let btnStyle =
                          'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 hover:border-violet-400 text-slate-800';
                        if (isPicked && isRight) {
                          btnStyle =
                            'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-900 ring-2 ring-emerald-200';
                        } else if (isPicked && !isRight) {
                          btnStyle =
                            'bg-rose-50 border-rose-500 text-rose-900 animate-shake';
                        }

                        return (
                          <button
                            key={optIdx}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              handleSelectQuiz(slide.id, optIdx, quizCorrectIdx)
                            }
                            className={`text-left p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 font-bold transition flex items-center gap-3 ${
                              boardMode ? 'text-base sm:text-lg' : 'text-sm'
                            } ${btnStyle}`}
                          >
                            <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-600 shrink-0">
                              {String.fromCharCode(65 + optIdx)}
                            </span>
                            <span className="flex-1">
                              <MathMarkdown content={opt} inline />
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>

                    {chosenQuizOpt !== undefined && (
                      <div
                        className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold animate-pop ${
                          chosenQuizOpt === quizCorrectIdx
                            ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-900'
                            : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 text-amber-900'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="font-extrabold mb-0.5">
                          {chosenQuizOpt === quizCorrectIdx
                            ? '🎉 Точно в цель!'
                            : '🤔 Почти! Попробуй другой вариант.'}
                        </div>
                        {chosenQuizOpt === quizCorrectIdx &&
                          slide.quiz_explanation && (
                            <MathMarkdown content={slide.quiz_explanation} />
                          )}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

              {slide.takeaways && slide.takeaways.length > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
                  {slide.takeaways.map((item, i) => (
                    <div
                      key={i}
                      className="flex items-start gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-200"
                    >
                      <span className="text-emerald-600 font-extrabold text-base leading-none mt-0.5">
                        ✓
                      </span>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <MathMarkdown content={item} large={boardMode} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="pt-5 mt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={goPrev}
                disabled={safeIndex === 0}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 disabled:opacity-35 text-slate-700 text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition"
              >
                ← Назад
              </button>

              <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
                <span>{progressPct}% изучено</span>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={goNext}
                className={`btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-white transition ${
                  safeIndex === slides.length - 1
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800'
                    : 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800'
                }`}
              >
                {safeIndex === slides.length - 1
                  ? '⭐ Завершить слайды'
                  : 'Дальше →'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {completedDeck && (
            <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-teal-500 to-indigo-600 text-white p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3.5 shadow-md animate-pop">
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <div className="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold">
                  🏆 Звезда за теорию зажжена!
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-emerald-50">
                  Отличный темп! Теперь закрепи тему в интерактивном тренажёре.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setCompletedDeck(false);
                    setIndex(0);
                  }}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white text-xs font-extrabold transition"
                >
                  ↻ С начала
                </button>
                {onGoToPractice && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onGoToPractice}
                    className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200 text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold shadow-xs"
                  >
                    🎮 Перейти к тренажёру →
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {hasRichSlides ? (
            slides.map((s, i) => (
              <div
                key={s.id}
                className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/70 border border-slate-200 p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3"
              >
                <div className="text-xs font-extrabold text-indigo-600">
                  {i + 1}. {SLIDE_META[s.type]?.icon} {s.title}
                </div>
                {s.body_md && <MathMarkdown content={s.body_md} large={boardMode} />}
                {s.visual && <SlideVisualBox visual={s.visual} large={boardMode} />}
                {s.steps && s.steps.length > 0 && (
                  <ol className="list-decimal pl-5 space-y-1.5 text-sm text-slate-700">
                    {s.steps.map((st, idx) => (
                      <li key={idx}>
                        <MathMarkdown content={st} inline />
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ol>
                )}
                {s.takeaways && s.takeaways.length > 0 && (
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
                    {s.takeaways.map((tk, idx) => (
                      <li key={idx}>
                        ✓ <MathMarkdown content={tk} inline />
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <MathMarkdown content={topic.theory_md || ''} large={boardMode} />
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/MaterialPreviewModal.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect } from 'react';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  url: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

function resolveEmbed(rawUrl: string): {
  mode: 'image' | 'iframe';
  embedUrl: string;
  hint?: string;
} {
  const url = rawUrl.trim();
  const lower = url.toLowerCase();

  // Картинки
  if (/\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|svg)(\?|$)/i.test(lower)) {
    return { mode: 'image', embedUrl: url };
  }

  // Файлы PowerPoint, Word, Excel (из Supabase Storage или по прямой ссылке)
  if (/\.(pptx?|docx?|xlsx?)(\?|$)/i.test(lower)) {
    return {
      mode: 'iframe',
      embedUrl: `https://view.officeapps.live.com/op/embed.aspx?src=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`,
      hint: 'Просмотр документа Office · если файл грузится долго, нажми «Открыть отдельно ↗»',
    };
  }

  // YouTube (обычные ссылки, youtu.be, shorts)
  const ytMatch = url.match(
    /(?:youtube\.com\/(?:watch\?v=|embed\/|shorts\/)|youtu\.be\/)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{11})/
  );
  if (ytMatch?.[1]) {
    return {
      mode: 'iframe',
      embedUrl: `https://www.youtube.com/embed/${ytMatch[1]}`,
    };
  }

  // Google Slides / Docs / Drive
  if (lower.includes('docs.google.com/presentation/d/')) {
    const clean = url.replace(
      /\/(edit|view).*$/,
      '/embed?start=false&loop=false'
    );
    return { mode: 'iframe', embedUrl: clean };
  }
  if (lower.includes('docs.google.com/document/d/')) {
    const clean = url.replace(/\/(edit|view).*$/, '/preview');
    return { mode: 'iframe', embedUrl: clean };
  }
  if (lower.includes('drive.google.com/file/d/')) {
    const clean = url.replace(/\/(view|edit).*$/, '/preview');
    return { mode: 'iframe', embedUrl: clean };
  }

  // Canva
  if (lower.includes('canva.com/design/') && !lower.includes('embed')) {
    const sep = url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
    return { mode: 'iframe', embedUrl: `${url}${sep}embed` };
  }

  // PDF, GeoGebra и остальные embed-ссылки
  return { mode: 'iframe', embedUrl: url };
}

export default function MaterialPreviewModal({ title, url, onClose }: Props) {
  const { mode, embedUrl, hint } = resolveEmbed(url);

  useEffect(() => {
    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div
      onClick={onClose}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-5"
    >
      <div
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="w-full max-w-5xl h-[86vh] bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden animate-pop"
      >
        <div className="px-4 sm:px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-indigo-600">
              {hint || 'Встроенный просмотр'}
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 truncate">
              {title}
            </h3>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            <a
              href={url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-50 text-slate-700 hover:text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold transition"
            >
              Открыть отдельно ↗
            </a>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-700 text-slate-600 font-extrabold transition"
              title="Закрыть"
            >
              ✕
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 bg-slate-100 overflow-auto flex items-center justify-center">
          {mode === 'image' ? (
            <img
              src={embedUrl}
              alt={title}
              className="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain p-3"
            />
          ) : (
            <iframe
              src={embedUrl}
              title={title}
              className="w-full h-full border-0 bg-white"
              allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; fullscreen"
              allowFullScreen
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/MathMarkdown.tsx =====
'use client';

import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkMath from 'remark-math';
import rehypeKatex from 'rehype-katex';

interface Props {
  content: string;
  large?: boolean;
  inline?: boolean;
}

export default function MathMarkdown({
  content,
  large = false,
  inline = false,
}: Props) {
  if (inline) {
    return (
      <span className="inline leading-snug">
        <ReactMarkdown
          remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]}
          rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}
          components={{
            p: ({ children }) => <span>{children}</span>,
            strong: ({ children }) => <strong className="font-bold">{children}</strong>,
          }}
        >
          {content || ''}
        </ReactMarkdown>
      </span>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`space-y-2 text-slate-700 leading-relaxed ${
        large ? 'text-base sm:text-lg lg:text-xl' : 'text-sm sm:text-[15px]'
      }`}
    >
      <ReactMarkdown
        remarkPlugins={[remarkMath]}
        rehypePlugins={[rehypeKatex]}
        components={{
          h3: ({ children }) => (
            <h3
              className={`font-bold text-slate-900 mt-5 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 ${
                large ? 'text-lg sm:text-xl lg:text-2xl' : 'text-base sm:text-lg'
              }`}
            >
              <span className="w-1.5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-500 inline-block shrink-0" />
              <span>{children}</span>
            </h3>
          ),
          ul: ({ children }) => (
            <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1.5 my-2">{children}</ul>
          ),
          ol: ({ children }) => (
            <ol className="list-decimal pl-5 space-y-1.5 my-2">{children}</ol>
          ),
          strong: ({ children }) => (
            <strong className="font-bold text-slate-900">{children}</strong>
          ),
          p: ({ children }) => <p className="my-2">{children}</p>,
        }}
      >
        {content?.trim() ? content : '*Конспект пока не заполнен.*'}
      </ReactMarkdown>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/ScheduleTopicModal.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { getLocalDateISO } from '@/lib/format/cleanTopic';
import type { ClassScheduleItem, Topic } from '@/types/db';

interface ClassroomOption {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  join_code: string;
}

interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  classrooms: ClassroomOption[];
  schedules: ClassScheduleItem[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: (item: ClassScheduleItem) => void;
  onDeleted: (classroomId: string, topicId: string) => void;
}

export default function ScheduleTopicModal({
  topic,
  classrooms,
  schedules,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
  onDeleted,
}: Props) {
  const today = getLocalDateISO(0);
  const tomorrow = getLocalDateISO(1);
  const dayAfter = getLocalDateISO(2);

  const [classroomId, setClassroomId] = useState(classrooms[0]?.id || '');

  const existing = schedules.find(
    (s) => s.classroom_id === classroomId && s.topic_id === topic.id
  );

  const [lessonDate, setLessonDate] = useState(existing?.lesson_date || today);
  const [hwDueDate, setHwDueDate] = useState(existing?.hw_due_date || tomorrow);
  const [note, setNote] = useState(existing?.note || '');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  function handleClassChange(nextClassId: string) {
    setClassroomId(nextClassId);
    const found = schedules.find(
      (s) => s.classroom_id === nextClassId && s.topic_id === topic.id
    );
    setLessonDate(found?.lesson_date || today);
    setHwDueDate(found?.hw_due_date || tomorrow);
    setNote(found?.note || '');
  }

  async function handleSave(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!classroomId || !lessonDate) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/teacher/schedule', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          classroomId,
          topicId: topic.id,
          lessonDate,
          hwDueDate: hwDueDate || null,
          note,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        alert(data.error || 'Ошибка сохранения');
        return;
      }
      onSaved(data.item as ClassScheduleItem);
      onClose();
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleRemove() {
    if (!classroomId) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(
        `/api/teacher/schedule?classroomId=${classroomId}&topicId=${topic.id}`,
        { method: 'DELETE' }
      );
      if (res.ok) {
        onDeleted(classroomId, topic.id);
        onClose();
      }
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div
      onClick={onClose}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
    >
      <form
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        onSubmit={handleSave}
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4 animate-pop"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900">
              📅 Назначить урок классу
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 line-clamp-1">
              {topic.title}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-bold"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        {classrooms.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-xs text-amber-900">
            Сначала создай хотя бы один класс (например, 5 «А») в разделе{' '}
            <strong>📊 Журнал → Классы и ученики</strong>.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Выбери класс
              </label>
              <select
                value={classroomId}
                onChange={(e) => handleClassChange(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              >
                {classrooms.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                    {c.name} ({c.join_code})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                Когда этот урок?
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setLessonDate(today);
                    setHwDueDate(tomorrow);
                  }}
                  className={`py-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                    lessonDate === today
                      ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-800'
                      : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:border-emerald-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  🟢 На сегодня
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setLessonDate(tomorrow);
                    setHwDueDate(dayAfter);
                  }}
                  className={`py-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                    lessonDate === tomorrow
                      ? 'bg-sky-50 border-sky-500 text-sky-800'
                      : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:border-sky-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  🔵 На завтра
                </button>
              </div>
              <input
                type="date"
                required
                value={lessonDate}
                onChange={(e) => setLessonDate(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Срок сдачи ДЗ (необязательно)
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                value={hwDueDate}
                onChange={(e) => setHwDueDate(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Короткое сообщение классу (необязательно)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={note}
                onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Например: Повторите признаки делимости перед уроком!"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
              {existing ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={saving}
                  onClick={handleRemove}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 text-xs font-bold transition"
                >
                  Убрать дату
                </button>
              ) : (
                <div />
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving}
                className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs font-extrabold"
              >
                {saving ? 'Сохранение...' : '✓ Назначить классу'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/SlideStudioPanel.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { playSound } from '@/lib/audio/sound';
import type { LessonSlide, SlideType, SlideVisual, Topic } from '@/types/db';

interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  onTopicUpdated: (updated: Topic) => void;
}

const TYPE_OPTIONS: { value: SlideType; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'intro', label: '🚀 Зачем это нужно' },
  { value: 'concept', label: '💡 Главное правило' },
  { value: 'example', label: '👀 Пошаговый разбор' },
  { value: 'trap', label: '⚠️ Ловушка (Ошибка vs Верно)' },
  { value: 'quiz', label: '🎯 Мини-проверка' },
  { value: 'summary', label: '⭐ Шпаргалка урока' },
];

const VISUAL_OPTIONS: { value: '' | SlideVisual['kind']; label: string }[] = [
  { value: '', label: 'Без схемы' },
  { value: 'formula_hero', label: '✨ Крупная формула' },
  { value: 'fraction_bar', label: '🍕 Плитки дробей' },
  { value: 'number_line', label: '📏 Координатный луч' },
  { value: 'percent_grid', label: '💯 Сетка процентов (10×10)' },
  { value: 'angle', label: '📐 Угол с дугой' },
  { value: 'venn', label: '⭕ Круги Эйлера–Венна' },
  { value: 'compare', label: '⚖️ Сравнение 2 карточек' },
];

function createDefaultVisual(kind: SlideVisual['kind']): SlideVisual {
  switch (kind) {
    case 'formula_hero':
      return { kind: 'formula_hero', formula: '$a + b = b + a$', caption: 'Главное правило' };
    case 'fraction_bar':
      return { kind: 'fraction_bar', numerator: 3, denominator: 4, label: 'Дробь 3/4' };
    case 'number_line':
      return {
        kind: 'number_line',
        min: 0,
        max: 10,
        points: [
          { value: 3, label: 'A(3)', highlight: true },
          { value: 7, label: 'B(7)', highlight: true },
        ],
        jump: { from: 3, to: 7, label: '+4' },
      };
    case 'percent_grid':
      return { kind: 'percent_grid', percent: 35, label: '35% = 0,35' };
    case 'angle':
      return { kind: 'angle', degrees: 60, label: 'Острый угол меньше 90°' };
    case 'venn':
      return {
        kind: 'venn',
        leftLabel: 'Множество A',
        rightLabel: 'Множество B',
        leftItems: ['2', '4'],
        bothItems: ['6', '12'],
        rightItems: ['3', '9'],
      };
    case 'compare':
      return {
        kind: 'compare',
        leftTitle: 'Случай 1',
        leftText: 'Описание первого случая',
        rightTitle: 'Случай 2',
        rightText: 'Описание второго случая',
      };
  }
}

export default function SlideStudioPanel({ topic, onTopicUpdated }: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [slides, setSlides] = useState<LessonSlide[]>(
    Array.isArray(topic.slides) ? topic.slides : []
  );
  const [wish, setWish] = useState('');
  const [generating, setGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [openEditor, setOpenEditor] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSlides(Array.isArray(topic.slides) ? topic.slides : []);
    setError('');
  }, [topic.id, topic.slides]);

  async function saveSlidesToDb(nextSlides: LessonSlide[]) {
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const { data, error: dbErr } = await supabase
        .from('topics')
        .update({ slides: nextSlides })
        .eq('id', topic.id)
        .select()
        .single();

      if (dbErr || !data) {
        setError(dbErr?.message || 'Не удалось сохранить слайды');
        return false;
      }

      playSound('success');
