      onTopicUpdated(data as Topic);
      return true;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleGenerate() {
    setGenerating(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/teacher/generate-slides', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ topicId: topic.id, extraWish: wish }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || 'Ошибка генерации презентации');
        return;
      }
      const generated = (data.slides || []) as LessonSlide[];
      setSlides(generated);
      await saveSlidesToDb(generated);
      setOpenEditor(true);
    } catch {
      setError('Сетевая ошибка при обращении к ИИ.');
    } finally {
      setGenerating(false);
    }
  }

  function updateSlide(idx: number, patch: Partial<LessonSlide>) {
    setSlides((prev) =>
      prev.map((s, i) => (i === idx ? { ...s, ...patch } : s))
    );
  }

  function moveSlide(idx: number, dir: -1 | 1) {
    const target = idx + dir;
    if (target < 0 || target >= slides.length) return;
    setSlides((prev) => {
      const copy = [...prev];
      const [moved] = copy.splice(idx, 1);
      copy.splice(target, 0, moved);
      return copy;
    });
  }

  function addBlankSlide() {
    const next: LessonSlide = {
      id: `manual-${Date.now()}`,
      type: 'concept',
      title: `Новый слайд ${slides.length + 1}`,
      body_md: 'Краткое объяснение с формулой $a + b = b + a$.',
    };
    setSlides((prev) => [...prev, next]);
    setOpenEditor(true);
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-50 via-white to-indigo-50 border-2 border-violet-200 p-5 space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h4 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-violet-950">
            ✨ Студия интерактивных слайдов (ИИ)
          </h4>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">
            Gemini соберёт 6–7 наглядных слайдов со схемами, пошаговым примером, ловушкой и 2 мини-тестами.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setOpenEditor((v) => !v)}
          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-violet-200 text-xs font-bold text-violet-800 hover:bg-violet-50"
        >
          {openEditor
            ? 'Скрыть редактор слайдов'
            : `🛠️ Редактировать слайды (${slides.length})`}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={wish}
          onChange={(e) => setWish(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Пожелание (напр.: добавь схему пиццы дробей и задачу про покупки в тенге)"
          className="flex-1 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-white border border-violet-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500"
        />
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={generating || saving}
          onClick={handleGenerate}
          className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-700 border-violet-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold shrink-0"
        >
          {generating
            ? '✨ Собираем слайды...'
            : slides.length > 0
              ? '✨ Пересобрать через ИИ'
              : '✨ Собрать презентацию (ИИ)'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs font-bold text-rose-700">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {openEditor && (
        <div className="space-y-3 pt-3 border-t border-violet-200/70">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={addBlankSlide}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-indigo-200 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold hover:bg-indigo-50"
            >
              + Добавить слайд
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={saving}
              onClick={() => saveSlidesToDb(slides)}
              className="btn-tactile px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 border-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs font-extrabold"
            >
              {saving ? 'Сохраняем...' : `💾 Сохранить изменения (${slides.length})`}
            </button>
          </div>

          {slides.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-300 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
              Слайдов пока нет — нажми «✨ Собрать презентацию (ИИ)» выше или «+ Добавить слайд».
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {slides.map((s, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={s.id}
                  className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4 space-y-2.5"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-700">
                        #{idx + 1}
                      </span>
                      <select
                        value={s.type}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          updateSlide(idx, { type: e.target.value as SlideType })
                        }
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold"
                      >
                        {TYPE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                          <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                            {opt.label}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>

                      <select
                        value={s.visual?.kind || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          const val = e.target.value as '' | SlideVisual['kind'];
                          updateSlide(idx, {
                            visual: val ? createDefaultVisual(val) : null,
                          });
                        }}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 text-indigo-800 text-xs font-bold"
                        title="Встроенная схема слайда"
                      >
                        {VISUAL_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                          <option key={opt.value || 'none'} value={opt.value}>
                            📊 {opt.label}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => moveSlide(idx, -1)}
                        disabled={idx === 0}
                        className="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-30 text-xs font-bold"
                        title="Выше"
                      >
                        ↑
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => moveSlide(idx, 1)}
                        disabled={idx === slides.length - 1}
                        className="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-30 text-xs font-bold"
                        title="Ниже"
                      >
                        ↓
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setSlides((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
                        }
                        className="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 text-xs font-bold"
                        title="Удалить слайд"
                      >
                        🗑️
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={s.title}
                    onChange={(e) => updateSlide(idx, { title: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Заголовок слайда"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
                  />

                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={s.body_md}
                    onChange={(e) => updateSlide(idx, { body_md: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Основной текст (поддерживает формулы $LaTeX$)"
                    className="w-full p-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
                  />

                  {/* Редактор параметров встроенной SVG-схемы */}
                  {s.visual && (
                    <VisualMiniEditor
                      visual={s.visual}
                      onChange={(nextVisual) =>
                        updateSlide(idx, { visual: nextVisual })
                      }
                    />
                  )}

                  {s.type === 'example' && (
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      value={(s.steps || []).join('\n')}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        updateSlide(idx, {
                          steps: e.target.value
                            .split('\n')
                            .map((line) => line.trim())
                            .filter(Boolean),
                        })
                      }
                      placeholder="Шаги решения (каждый шаг с новой строки)"
                      className="w-full p-2.5 text-xs bg-emerald-50/40 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl"
                    />
                  )}

                  {s.type === 'trap' && (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={s.wrong_md || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateSlide(idx, { wrong_md: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="❌ Как часто ошибаются"
                        className="px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-rose-50/50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl"
                      />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={s.right_md || ''}
                        onChange={(e) => updateSlide(idx, { right_md: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="✅ Как правильно"
                        className="px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-emerald-50/50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl"
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {s.type === 'quiz' && (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={(s.quiz_options || []).join('; ')}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          updateSlide(idx, {
                            quiz_options: e.target.value
                              .split(';')
                              .map((v) => v.trim())
                              .filter(Boolean),
                          })
                        }
                        placeholder="Варианты ответа через ; (например: 12; 15; 18; 24)"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-violet-50/40 border border-violet-200 rounded-xl"
                      />
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min={1}
                          max={4}
                          value={(s.quiz_correct_index ?? 0) + 1}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            updateSlide(idx, {
                              quiz_correct_index: Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value) - 1),
                            })
                          }
                          placeholder="№ верного (1-4)"
                          className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
                        />
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={s.quiz_explanation || ''}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            updateSlide(idx, { quiz_explanation: e.target.value })
                          }
                          placeholder="Пояснение после верного ответа"
                          className="sm:col-span-2 px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {s.type === 'summary' && (
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      value={(s.takeaways || []).join('\n')}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        updateSlide(idx, {
                          takeaways: e.target.value
                            .split('\n')
                            .map((line) => line.trim())
                            .filter(Boolean),
                        })
                      }
                      placeholder="Главные выводы шпаргалки (по одному на строку)"
                      className="w-full p-2.5 text-xs bg-indigo-50/40 border border-indigo-200 rounded-xl"
                    />
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function VisualMiniEditor({
  visual,
  onChange,
}: {
  visual: SlideVisual;
  onChange: (next: SlideVisual) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50/50 border border-indigo-200/80 space-y-2">
      <div className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-indigo-700 uppercase">
        Настройка схемы ({visual.kind})
      </div>

      {visual.kind === 'formula_hero' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={visual.formula}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, formula: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="Формула (напр. $S = a \cdot b$)"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={visual.caption || ''}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, caption: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="Пояснение под формулой"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {visual.kind === 'fraction_bar' && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              Числитель
              <input
                type="number"
                min={0}
                max={16}
                value={visual.numerator}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onChange({ ...visual, numerator: Number(e.target.value) || 0 })
                }
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              Знаменатель
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}
                max={16}
                value={visual.denominator}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onChange({
                    ...visual,
                    denominator: Math.max(1, Number(e.target.value) || 1),
                  })
                }
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="col-span-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              Подпись
              <input
                type="text"
                value={visual.label || ''}
                onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, label: e.target.value })}
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-800 cursor-pointer">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={visual.compareDenominator !== undefined}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  if (e.target.checked) {
                    onChange({
                      ...visual,
                      compareNumerator: 1,
                      compareDenominator: visual.denominator,
                    });
                  } else {
                    const { compareNumerator: _n, compareDenominator: _d, ...rest } =
                      visual;
                    onChange(rest);
                  }
                }}
              />
              Сравнить со второй дробью
            </label>

            {visual.compareDenominator !== undefined && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold">
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={16}
                  value={visual.compareNumerator ?? 1}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    onChange({
                      ...visual,
                      compareNumerator: Number(e.target.value) || 0,
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-14 px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-emerald-300 rounded-lg"
                />
                <span>/</span>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={16}
                  value={visual.compareDenominator}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    onChange({
                      ...visual,
                      compareDenominator: Math.max(1, Number(e.target.value) || 1),
                    })
                  }
                  className="w-14 px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-emerald-300 rounded-lg"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {visual.kind === 'percent_grid' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
            Процент (0–100)
            <input
              type="number"
              min={0}
              max={100}
              value={visual.percent}
              onChange={(e) =>
                onChange({ ...visual, percent: Number(e.target.value) || 0 })
              }
              className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="sm:col-span-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
            Пояснение
            <input
              type="text"
              value={visual.label || ''}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, label: e.target.value })}
              className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      )}

      {visual.kind === 'angle' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
            Градусы (5–355°)
            <input
              type="number"
              min={5}
              max={355}
              value={visual.degrees}
              onChange={(e) =>
                onChange({ ...visual, degrees: Number(e.target.value) || 60 })
              }
              className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="sm:col-span-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
            Подпись
            <input
              type="text"
              value={visual.label || ''}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, label: e.target.value })}
              className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      )}

      {visual.kind === 'number_line' && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-2">
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              От (min)
              <input
                type="number"
                value={visual.min}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onChange({ ...visual, min: Number(e.target.value) || 0 })
                }
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              До (max)
              <input
                type="number"
                value={visual.max}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onChange({ ...visual, max: Number(e.target.value) || 10 })
                }
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              Прыжок от
              <input
                type="number"
                value={visual.jump?.from ?? ''}
                placeholder="—"
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const val = e.target.value;
                  if (val === '') {
                    const { jump: _j, ...rest } = visual;
                    onChange(rest);
                  } else {
                    onChange({
                      ...visual,
                      jump: {
                        from: Number(val),
                        to: visual.jump?.to ?? visual.max,
                        label: visual.jump?.label ?? '',
                      },
                    });
                  }
                }}
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              Прыжок до
              <input
                type="number"
                value={visual.jump?.to ?? ''}
                placeholder="—"
                onChange={(e) => {
                  if (!visual.jump && e.target.value === '') return;
                  onChange({
                    ...visual,
                    jump: {
                      from: visual.jump?.from ?? visual.min,
                      to: Number(e.target.value) || 0,
                      label: visual.jump?.label ?? '',
                    },
                  });
                }}
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
              Подпись дуги
              <input
                type="text"
                value={visual.jump?.label ?? ''}
                placeholder="+4"
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onChange({
                    ...visual,
                    jump: {
                      from: visual.jump?.from ?? visual.min,
                      to: visual.jump?.to ?? visual.max,
                      label: e.target.value,
                    },
                  })
                }
                className="mt-0.5 w-full px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600">
                Точки на луче
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onChange({
                    ...visual,
                    points: [
                      ...(visual.points || []),
                      { value: visual.min, label: 'P', highlight: true },
                    ],
                  })
                }
                className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-white border border-indigo-200 text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-700"
              >
                + Точка
              </button>
            </div>
            {(visual.points || []).map((pt, pIdx) => (
              <div key={pIdx} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="any"
                  value={pt.value}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const nextPts = visual.points.map((p, i) =>
                      i === pIdx ? { ...p, value: Number(e.target.value) || 0 } : p
                    );
                    onChange({ ...visual, points: nextPts });
                  }}
                  className="w-20 px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
                />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={pt.label}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const nextPts = visual.points.map((p, i) =>
                      i === pIdx ? { ...p, label: e.target.value } : p
                    );
                    onChange({ ...visual, points: nextPts });
                  }}
                  placeholder="Подпись (напр. A(3))"
                  className="flex-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    onChange({
                      ...visual,
                      points: visual.points.filter((_, i) => i !== pIdx),
                    })
                  }
                  className="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 text-xs font-bold"
                >
                  ✕
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {visual.kind === 'venn' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={visual.leftLabel}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, leftLabel: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="Левый круг (название)"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={visual.rightLabel}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, rightLabel: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="Правый круг (название)"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg"
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={(visual.leftItems || []).join(', ')}
            onChange={(e) =>
              onChange({
                ...visual,
                leftItems: e.target.value
                  .split(',')
                  .map((x) => x.trim())
                  .filter(Boolean),
              })
            }
            placeholder="Только слева (через запятую)"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={(visual.rightItems || []).join(', ')}
            onChange={(e) =>
              onChange({
                ...visual,
                rightItems: e.target.value
                  .split(',')
                  .map((x) => x.trim())
                  .filter(Boolean),
              })
            }
            placeholder="Только справа (через запятую)"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={(visual.bothItems || []).join(', ')}
            onChange={(e) =>
              onChange({
                ...visual,
                bothItems: e.target.value
                  .split(',')
                  .map((x) => x.trim())
                  .filter(Boolean),
              })
            }
            placeholder="Общее в центре (через запятую)"
            className="sm:col-span-2 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {visual.kind === 'compare' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={visual.leftTitle}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, leftTitle: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Заголовок слева"
              className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={visual.leftText}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, leftText: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Текст слева"
              className="w-full p-2 text-xs bg-white border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={visual.rightTitle}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, rightTitle: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Заголовок справа"
              className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={visual.rightText}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...visual, rightText: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Текст справа"
              className="w-full p-2 text-xs bg-white border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/SlideVisualBox.tsx =====
'use client';

import MathMarkdown from './MathMarkdown';
import type { SlideVisual } from '@/types/db';

interface Props {
  visual: SlideVisual;
  large?: boolean;
}

function clamp(n: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return min;
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
}

export default function SlideVisualBox({ visual, large = false }: Props) {
  if (visual.kind === 'formula_hero') {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-600 via-violet-600 to-fuchsia-600 p-5 sm:p-6 text-white text-center shadow-sm space-y-2">
        <div
          className={`font-extrabold [&_p]:text-white [&_strong]:text-white ${
            large ? 'text-2xl sm:text-3xl' : 'text-xl sm:text-2xl'
          }`}
        >
          <MathMarkdown content={visual.formula} inline />
        </div>
        {visual.caption && (
          <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-indigo-100 font-semibold">
            {visual.caption}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (visual.kind === 'fraction_bar') {
    const den = Math.round(clamp(visual.denominator, 1, 16));
    const num = Math.round(clamp(visual.numerator, 0, den));
    const hasCompare =
      typeof visual.compareDenominator === 'number' &&
      typeof visual.compareNumerator === 'number';
    const cDen = hasCompare
      ? Math.round(clamp(visual.compareDenominator!, 1, 16))
      : 1;
    const cNum = hasCompare
      ? Math.round(clamp(visual.compareNumerator!, 0, cDen))
      : 0;

    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3">
        {visual.label && (
          <div className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-600 text-center">
            {visual.label}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
            <span>
              Закрашено {num} из {den} равных частей
            </span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 font-extrabold">
              {num}/{den}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div
            className="grid gap-1.5"
            style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${den}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}
          >
            {Array.from({ length: den }, (_, i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className={`h-11 sm:h-12 rounded-xl border-2 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                  i < num
                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-500 to-violet-600 border-indigo-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                {i < num ? '1/' + den : ''}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {hasCompare && (
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/80">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
              <span>
                Сравним: {cNum} из {cDen} частей
              </span>
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-extrabold">
                {cNum}/{cDen}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div
              className="grid gap-1.5"
              style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${cDen}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}
            >
              {Array.from({ length: cDen }, (_, i) => (
                <div
                  key={i}
                  className={`h-11 sm:h-12 rounded-xl border-2 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                    i < cNum
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 border-emerald-700 text-white shadow-xs'
                      : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-400'
                  }`}
                >
                  {i < cNum ? '1/' + cDen : ''}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (visual.kind === 'number_line') {
    const min = Number.isFinite(visual.min) ? visual.min : 0;
    const rawMax = Number.isFinite(visual.max) ? visual.max : 10;
    const max = rawMax > min ? rawMax : min + 10;
    const span = max - min;
    const toX = (v: number) => 36 + ((clamp(v, min, max) - min) / span) * 448;

    const tickCount = Math.min(10, Math.max(4, Math.round(span)));
    const ticks = Array.from({ length: tickCount + 1 }, (_, i) => {
      const val = min + (span * i) / tickCount;
      return Number(val.toFixed(1));
    });

    const jump = visual.jump;
    const jFromX = jump ? toX(jump.from) : 0;
    const jToX = jump ? toX(jump.to) : 0;
    const jMidX = (jFromX + jToX) / 2;

    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 overflow-x-auto">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 520 120" className="w-full min-w-[340px] max-w-xl mx-auto">
          {/* Луч и стрелка */}
          <g className="text-slate-600">
            <line
              x1={28}
              y1={72}
              x2={498}
              y2={72}
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth={3}
              strokeLinecap="round"
            />
            <polygon points="502,72 490,66 490,78" fill="currentColor" />
          </g>

          {/* Штрихи и подписи */}
          {ticks.map((val, idx) => {
            const x = toX(val);
            return (
              <g key={idx} className="text-slate-500">
                <line
                  x1={x}
                  y1={65}
                  x2={x}
                  y2={79}
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth={2}
                />
                <text
                  x={x}
                  y={98}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  fontSize={11}
                  fontWeight={700}
                  fill="currentColor"
                >
                  {val}
                </text>
              </g>
            );
          })}

          {/* Дуга прыжка */}
          {jump && (
            <g>
              <path
                d={`M ${jFromX} 64 Q ${jMidX} 16 ${jToX} 64`}
                fill="none"
                stroke="#10B981"
                strokeWidth={3}
                strokeDasharray="6 4"
              />
              {jump.label && (
                <text
                  x={jMidX}
                  y={24}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  fontSize={12}
                  fontWeight={800}
                  fill="#10B981"
                >
                  {jump.label}
                </text>
              )}
            </g>
          )}

          {/* Выделенные точки */}
          {(visual.points || []).map((pt, i) => {
            const x = toX(pt.value);
            const hl = pt.highlight !== false;
            return (
              <g key={i}>
                <circle
                  cx={x}
                  cy={72}
                  r={hl ? 7 : 5}
                  fill={hl ? '#6366F1' : '#0EA5E9'}
                  stroke="#FFFFFF"
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                />
                <text
                  x={x}
                  y={52}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  fontSize={12}
                  fontWeight={800}
                  fill={hl ? '#6366F1' : '#0EA5E9'}
                >
                  {pt.label}
                </text>
              </g>
            );
          })}
        </svg>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (visual.kind === 'percent_grid') {
    const pct = Math.round(clamp(visual.percent, 0, 100));
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-5">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-10 gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shrink-0">
          {Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 sm:w-4 sm:h-4 rounded-[3px] ${
                i < pct ? 'bg-indigo-600' : 'bg-slate-200'
              }`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center sm:text-left">
          <div className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-indigo-600 tabular-nums">
            {pct}%
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
            {pct} из 100 клеток ={' '}
            {String(Number((pct / 100).toFixed(2))).replace('.', ',')}
          </div>
          {visual.label && (
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{visual.label}</div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (visual.kind === 'angle') {
    const deg = Math.round(clamp(visual.degrees, 5, 355));
    const cx = 130;
    const cy = 125;
    const r = 92;
    const arcR = 36;
    const rad = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(-rad);
    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(-rad);
    const ax = cx + arcR * Math.cos(-rad);
    const ay = cy + arcR * Math.sin(-rad);
    const largeArc = deg > 180 ? 1 : 0;
    const midRad = (deg / 2) * (Math.PI / 180);
    const lx = cx + 54 * Math.cos(-midRad);
    const ly = cy + 54 * Math.sin(-midRad);

    const angleKind =
      deg < 90
        ? 'Острый угол'
        : deg === 90
          ? 'Прямой угол'
          : deg < 180
            ? 'Тупой угол'
            : deg === 180
              ? 'Развёрнутый угол'
              : 'Угол больше 180°';

    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-4">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 260 155" className="w-60 h-36 shrink-0">
          <path
            d={`M ${cx + arcR} ${cy} A ${arcR} ${arcR} 0 ${largeArc} 0 ${ax.toFixed(1)} ${ay.toFixed(1)}`}
            fill="none"
            stroke="#F59E0B"
            strokeWidth={4}
          />
          <line
            x1={cx}
            y1={cy}
            x2={cx + r}
            y2={cy}
            stroke="#6366F1"
            strokeWidth={3.5}
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <line
            x1={cx}
            y1={cy}
            x2={x2}
            y2={y2}
            stroke="#6366F1"
            strokeWidth={3.5}
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <circle
            cx={cx}
            cy={cy}
            r={5}
            className="text-slate-800"
            fill="currentColor"
          />
          <text
            x={lx}
            y={ly}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fontSize={13}
            fontWeight={800}
            fill="#F59E0B"
          >
            {deg}°
          </text>
        </svg>
        <div className="text-center sm:text-left space-y-1">
          <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">
            {deg}° — {angleKind}
          </div>
          {visual.label && (
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600">{visual.label}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (visual.kind === 'venn') {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 420 200" className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto">
          <circle
            cx={155}
            cy={108}
            r={74}
            fill="rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.22)"
            stroke="#6366F1"
            strokeWidth={2.5}
          />
          <circle
            cx={265}
            cy={108}
            r={74}
            fill="rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.22)"
            stroke="#10B981"
            strokeWidth={2.5}
          />

          <text
            x={115}
            y={24}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fontSize={13}
            fontWeight={800}
            fill="#6366F1"
          >
            {visual.leftLabel}
          </text>
          <text
            x={305}
            y={24}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fontSize={13}
            fontWeight={800}
            fill="#10B981"
          >
            {visual.rightLabel}
          </text>

          <g className="text-slate-800">
            {(visual.leftItems || []).slice(0, 4).map((it, i) => (
              <text
                key={i}
                x={122}
                y={88 + i * 20}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fontSize={12}
                fontWeight={700}
                fill="currentColor"
              >
                {it}
              </text>
            ))}
            {(visual.rightItems || []).slice(0, 4).map((it, i) => (
              <text
                key={i}
                x={298}
                y={88 + i * 20}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fontSize={12}
                fontWeight={700}
                fill="currentColor"
              >
                {it}
              </text>
            ))}
          </g>

          {(visual.bothItems || []).slice(0, 4).map((it, i) => (
            <text
              key={i}
              x={210}
              y={88 + i * 20}
              textAnchor="middle"
              fontSize={12}
              fontWeight={800}
              fill="#6366F1"
            >
              {it}
            </text>
          ))}
        </svg>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (visual.kind === 'compare') {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-200 p-4 space-y-1.5">
          <div className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-indigo-700">
            {visual.leftTitle}
          </div>
          <MathMarkdown content={visual.leftText} large={large} />
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-200 p-4 space-y-1.5">
          <div className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase text-emerald-700">
            {visual.rightTitle}
          </div>
          <MathMarkdown content={visual.rightText} large={large} />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return null;
}

===== src/components/lesson/TheoryTab.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import LessonSlidesPlayer from './LessonSlidesPlayer';
import SlideStudioPanel from './SlideStudioPanel';
import MaterialPreviewModal from './MaterialPreviewModal';
import type { Material, Topic } from '@/types/db';

interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  materials: Material[];
  canEdit: boolean;
  boardMode?: boolean;
  onTopicUpdated: (updated: Topic) => void;
  onMaterialsChanged: (next: Material[]) => void;
  onSlidesCompleted?: () => void;
  onGoToPractice?: () => void;
}

const KIND_META: Record<string, { label: string; icon: string; cls: string }> = {
  presentation: {
    label: 'Презентация',
    icon: '📊',
    cls: 'bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 border-orange-200',
  },
  pdf: {
    label: 'PDF / Документ',
    icon: '📄',
    cls: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200',
  },
  video: {
    label: 'Видеоурок',
    icon: '🎬',
    cls: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200',
  },
  link: {
    label: 'Ссылка',
    icon: '🔗',
    cls: 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border-sky-200',
  },
};

function buildMaterialStoragePath(topicId: string, fileName: string): string {
  const ext = fileName.split('.').pop() || 'bin';
  return `${topicId}/${Date.now()}.${ext}`;
}

export default function TheoryTab({
  topic,
  materials,
  canEdit,
  boardMode = false,
  onTopicUpdated,
  onMaterialsChanged,
  onSlidesCompleted,
  onGoToPractice,
}: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(topic.theory_md || '');
  const [savingTheory, setSavingTheory] = useState(false);

  const [kind, setKind] = useState<'presentation' | 'pdf' | 'video' | 'link'>(
    'presentation'
  );
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [previewItem, setPreviewItem] = useState<{
    title: string;
    url: string;
  } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setDraft(topic.theory_md || '');
    setEditing(false);
  }, [topic.id, topic.theory_md]);

  const visibleMaterials = materials.filter((m) => m.kind !== 'iframe_game');

  async function saveTheory() {
    setSavingTheory(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('topics')
      .update({ theory_md: draft })
      .eq('id', topic.id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (!error && data) {
      onTopicUpdated(data as Topic);
      setEditing(false);
    } else if (error) {
      alert('Ошибка сохранения конспекта: ' + error.message);
    }
    setSavingTheory(false);
  }

  async function handleAddMaterial(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim()) return;
    setUploading(true);

    let storagePath: string | null = null;
    let fileName: string | null = null;
    let finalUrl: string | null = url.trim() || null;

    if (file) {
      const safePath = buildMaterialStoragePath(topic.id, file.name);
      const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
        .from('lesson-materials')
        .upload(safePath, file);

      if (upErr) {
        alert('Ошибка загрузки файла: ' + upErr.message);
        setUploading(false);
        return;
      }

      storagePath = safePath;
      fileName = file.name;
      const { data: pub } = supabase.storage
        .from('lesson-materials')
        .getPublicUrl(safePath);
      finalUrl = pub.publicUrl;
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('materials')
      .insert({
        topic_id: topic.id,
        kind,
        title: title.trim(),
        description: desc.trim(),
        url: finalUrl,
        storage_path: storagePath,
        file_name: fileName,
      })
      .select()
      .single();

    if (!error && data) {
      onMaterialsChanged([...materials, data as Material]);
      setTitle('');
      setUrl('');
      setDesc('');
      setFile(null);
    } else if (error) {
      alert('Ошибка добавления материала: ' + error.message);
    }
    setUploading(false);
  }

  async function handleDeleteMaterial(m: Material) {
    if (m.storage_path) {
      await supabase.storage.from('lesson-materials').remove([m.storage_path]);
    }
    const { error } = await supabase.from('materials').delete().eq('id', m.id);
    if (!error) {
      onMaterialsChanged(materials.filter((item) => item.id !== m.id));
    }
  }

  // Если материалов нет и не включён редактор — отдаём всю ширину под слайды
  const hideEmptyMaterials = !canEdit && visibleMaterials.length === 0;

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
      {/* Основная колонка: Интерактивные слайды и конспект */}
      <div
        className={`${
          hideEmptyMaterials ? 'lg:col-span-3' : 'lg:col-span-2'
        } bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">
            📘 Урок и слайды
          </h3>
          {canEdit && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setDraft(topic.theory_md || '');
                setEditing((v) => !v);
              }}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-100 transition"
            >
              {editing ? 'Отмена' : '✏️ Текст конспекта'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {canEdit && editing ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Поддерживается Markdown и формулы LaTeX, например{' '}
              <code>{'$\\frac{3}{4} + \\sqrt{x}$'}</code>.
            </p>
            <textarea
              rows={14}
              value={draft}
              onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-3.5 text-sm font-mono bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 rounded-2xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
            />
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={savingTheory}
                onClick={saveTheory}
                className="btn-tactile px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition"
              >
                {savingTheory ? 'Сохранение...' : '💾 Сохранить конспект'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <LessonSlidesPlayer
              topic={topic}
              boardMode={boardMode}
              isTeacherView={canEdit}
              onSlidesCompleted={onSlidesCompleted}
              onGoToPractice={onGoToPractice}
            />
            {canEdit && (
              <SlideStudioPanel
                topic={topic}
                onTopicUpdated={onTopicUpdated}
              />
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Правая колонка: Файлы, презентации и видео */}
      {!hideEmptyMaterials && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-5 space-y-4 h-fit">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900">
              📎 Материалы к уроку
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
              {visibleMaterials.length}
            </span>
          </div>

          {visibleMaterials.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-6 px-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-dashed border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-500">
              Файлы и презентации к этой теме пока не добавлены.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {visibleMaterials.map((m) => {
                const meta = KIND_META[m.kind] || KIND_META.link;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={m.id}
                    className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <span
                        className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold border ${meta.cls}`}
                      >
                        {meta.icon} {meta.label}
                      </span>
                      {canEdit && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleDeleteMaterial(m)}
                          className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600"
                          title="Удалить материал"
                        >
                          ✕
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                      {m.title}
                    </div>
                    {m.description && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{m.description}</p>
                    )}
                    {m.url && (
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 pt-1">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            setPreviewItem({ title: m.title, url: m.url! })
                          }
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition"
                        >
                          👁️ Смотреть здесь
                        </button>
                        <a
                          href={m.url}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200 text-xs font-bold transition"
                          title="Открыть в новой вкладке"
                        >
                          {m.file_name ? '⬇ Скачать' : '↗'}
                        </a>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          {canEdit && (
            <form
              onSubmit={handleAddMaterial}
              className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2.5"
            >
              <div className="text-xs font-extrabold text-indigo-700 uppercase">
                + Загрузить материал
              </div>
              <select
                value={kind}
                onChange={(e) => setKind(e.target.value as typeof kind)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              >
                <option value="presentation">📊 Презентация (PPTX / PDF / Canva)</option>
                <option value="pdf">📄 Документ / PDF / DOCX</option>
                <option value="video">🎬 Видеоурок (YouTube / ссылка)</option>
                <option value="link">🔗 Полезная ссылка</option>
              </select>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={title}
                onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Название материала"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
              <input
                type="url"
                value={url}
                onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ссылка (или загрузи файл ниже)"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
              <input
                type="file"
                onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)}
                className="w-full text-[11px] text-slate-500 file:mr-2 file:py-1.5 file:px-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:bg-indigo-50 file:text-indigo-700 file:font-semibold"
              />
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={desc}
                onChange={(e) => setDesc(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Краткое описание..."
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={uploading}
                className="btn-tactile w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 border-indigo-800 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs font-extrabold transition"
              >
                {uploading ? 'Загрузка в облако...' : 'Прикрепить к уроку'}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Встроенный просмотрщик презентаций / PDF / видео */}
      {previewItem && (
        <MaterialPreviewModal
          title={previewItem.title}
          url={previewItem.url}
          onClose={() => setPreviewItem(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/lesson/TopicEditorModal.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import type { Topic } from '@/types/db';

interface Props {
  topic: Topic;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdated: (updated: Topic) => void;
  onDeleted: (topicId: string) => void;
}

const SIM_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'coord_ray', label: '📏 Координатный луч' },
  { value: 'divisibility', label: '⚡ Блиц: Признаки делимости' },
  { value: 'fractions_visual', label: '🍕 Пицца дробей' },
  { value: 'angles', label: '📐 Транспортир' },
  { value: 'sets', label: '⭕ Диаграммы Эйлера–Венна' },
  { value: 'percent_grid', label: '💯 Проценты (сетка 10×10)' },
  { value: 'diagrams', label: '📊 Конструктор диаграмм' },
  { value: 'natural', label: '🎲 Генератор: Натуральные числа' },
  { value: 'arithmetic', label: '🎲 Генератор: Арифметика' },
  { value: 'expressions', label: '🎲 Генератор: Буквенные выражения' },
  { value: 'equations', label: '🎲 Генератор: Уравнения' },
  { value: 'powers', label: '🎲 Генератор: Степени' },
  { value: 'fractions_calc', label: '🎲 Генератор: Обыкновенные дроби' },
  { value: 'decimals', label: '🎲 Генератор: Десятичные дроби' },
  { value: 'percents', label: '🎲 Генератор: Проценты' },
];

export default function TopicEditorModal({
  topic,
  onClose,
  onUpdated,
  onDeleted,
}: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [title, setTitle] = useState(topic.title);
  const [quarter, setQuarter] = useState<number>(topic.quarter);
  const [section, setSection] = useState(topic.section);
  const [orderIndex, setOrderIndex] = useState(topic.order_index);
  const [dates, setDates] = useState(topic.dates || '');
  const [hours, setHours] = useState(topic.hours || 1);
  const [simType, setSimType] = useState(topic.sim_type || 'arithmetic');
  const [isAssessment, setIsAssessment] = useState(Boolean(topic.is_assessment));
  const [valueExample, setValueExample] = useState(topic.value_example || '');
  const [objectivesText, setObjectivesText] = useState(
    Array.isArray(topic.objectives) ? topic.objectives.join('\n') : ''
  );
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  async function handleSave(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim()) return;
    setSaving(true);

    const objectives = objectivesText
      .split('\n')
      .map((s) => s.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('topics')
      .update({
        title: title.trim(),
        quarter,
        section: section.trim() || `Раздел ${quarter} четверти`,
        order_index: Math.max(1, orderIndex),
        dates: dates.trim(),
        hours: Math.max(1, hours),
        sim_type: simType,
        is_assessment: isAssessment,
        value_example: valueExample.trim(),
        objectives,
      })
      .eq('id', topic.id)
      .select()
      .single();

    setSaving(false);

    if (error || !data) {
      alert('Ошибка сохранения темы: ' + error?.message);
      return;
    }

    onUpdated(data as Topic);
    onClose();
  }

  async function handleDelete() {
    if (
      !confirm(
        `Удалить тему «${topic.title}» вместе с её материалами и домашними заданиями?`
      )
    ) {
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    const { error } = await supabase.from('topics').delete().eq('id', topic.id);
    setSaving(false);

    if (error) {
      alert('Ошибка удаления: ' + error.message);
      return;
    }

    onDeleted(topic.id);
    onClose();
  }

  return (
    <div
      onClick={onClose}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto"
    >
      <form
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        onSubmit={handleSave}
        className="w-full max-w-2xl bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4 my-auto"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">
            ⚙️ Настройки темы урока
          </h3>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-bold"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
          <div className="sm:col-span-3">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Название темы
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              № по порядку
            </label>
            <input
              type="number"
              min={1}
              value={orderIndex}
              onChange={(e) => setOrderIndex(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Четверть
            </label>
            <select
              value={quarter}
              onChange={(e) => setQuarter(Number(e.target.value) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            >
              {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((q) => (
                <option key={q} value={q}>
                  {q} четверть
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Даты (КТП)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={dates}
              onChange={(e) => setDates(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="02.09–04.09"
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Часов
            </label>
            <input
              type="number"
              min={1}
              max={20}
              value={hours}
              onChange={(e) => setHours(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-end pb-1">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl w-full">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={isAssessment}
                onChange={(e) => setIsAssessment(e.target.checked)}
                className="accent-amber-600"
              />
              СОР / СОЧ
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Раздел КТП
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={section}
              onChange={(e) => setSection(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Тренажёр по умолчанию
            </label>
            <select
              value={simType}
              onChange={(e) => setSimType(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
            >
              {SIM_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
                <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>
                  {o.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
            Контекст урока (жизненный пример)
          </label>
          <textarea
            rows={2}
            value={valueExample}
            onChange={(e) => setValueExample(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full p-3 text-xs sm:text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
            Цели обучения (каждая цель — с новой строки)
          </label>
          <textarea
            rows={3}
            value={objectivesText}
            onChange={(e) => setObjectivesText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="5.1.1.1 усвоить понятие множества натуральных чисел"
            className="w-full p-3 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={saving}
            onClick={handleDelete}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 text-xs font-bold transition"
          >
            🗑️ Удалить тему
          </button>

          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700"
            >
              Отмена
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-60 text-white text-xs font-bold transition"
            >
              {saving ? 'Сохранение...' : '💾 Сохранить изменения'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/CoordRaySim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

interface Props {
  onResult: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

const TICKS = Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => i);

export default function CoordRaySim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'point' | 'inequality'>('point');
  const [targetPoint, setTargetPoint] = useState(() => randInt(1, 11));
  const [ineq, setIneq] = useState(() => {
    const a = randInt(1, 5);
    return { a, b: Math.min(11, a + randInt(3, 5)) };
  });
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  function clearPendingTimer() {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => clearPendingTimer();
  }, []);

  function newTask(nextMode = mode) {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setSelected([]);
    setFeedback(null);
    if (nextMode === 'point') {
      setTargetPoint(randInt(1, 11));
    } else {
      const a = randInt(1, 5);
      setIneq({ a, b: Math.min(11, a + randInt(3, 5)) });
    }
  }

  function switchMode(nextMode: 'point' | 'inequality') {
    setMode(nextMode);
    newTask(nextMode);
  }

  function handleTickClick(val: number) {
    clearPendingTimer();
    if (mode === 'point') {
      setSelected([val]);
      if (val === targetPoint) {
        onResult(true);
        setFeedback({
          ok: true,
          text: `✓ Верно! Точка A(${targetPoint}) находится на ${targetPoint} ед. отрезках от нуля.`,
        });
        timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => newTask('point'), 1200);
      } else {
        onResult(false);
        const dir = val < targetPoint ? 'правее' : 'левее';
        setFeedback({
          ok: false,
          text: `✗ Ты нажал на ${val}. Число ${targetPoint} находится ${dir}! Попробуй ещё.`,
        });
      }
    } else {
      setFeedback(null);
      setSelected((prev) =>
        prev.includes(val) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== val) : [...prev, val]
      );
    }
  }

  function checkInequality() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const expected: number[] = [];
    for (let i = ineq.a + 1; i < ineq.b; i++) expected.push(i);
    const chosen = [...selected].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const ok =
      expected.length === chosen.length &&
      expected.every((v, idx) => v === chosen[idx]);

    onResult(ok);
    if (ok) {
      setFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Блестяще! Все натуральные решения (${expected.join(', ')}) найдены верно.`,
      });
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => newTask('inequality'), 1400);
    } else {
      setFeedback({
        ok: false,
        text: `✗ Не совсем точно. Помни: знаки строгие (<), границы ${ineq.a} и ${ineq.b} не входят!`,
      });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('point')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'point' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Поставь точку
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('inequality')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'inequality' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Двойное неравенство
          </button>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => newTask()}
          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition"
        >
          ↻ Новое задание
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200 text-center">
        {mode === 'point' ? (
          <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
            Кликни по лучу, чтобы отметить точку{' '}
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700">
              A({targetPoint})
            </span>
          </p>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
            Отметь на луче ВСЕ натуральные решения неравенства{' '}
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700">
              {ineq.a} &lt; x &lt; {ineq.b}
            </span>
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-5 border border-slate-200 overflow-x-auto">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 720 120" className="w-full min-w-[520px] h-auto select-none">
          {mode === 'inequality' && (
            <rect
              x={40 + ineq.a * 52}
              y={52}
              width={(ineq.b - ineq.a) * 52}
              height={16}
              rx={8}
              fill="rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.25)"
            />
          )}
          <g className="text-slate-700">
            <line
              x1={40}
              y1={60}
              x2={695}
              y2={60}
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth={3}
              strokeLinecap="round"
            />
            <polygon points="705,60 690,52 690,68" fill="currentColor" />
          </g>
          <text
            x={692}
            y={42}
            fontSize={14}
            fontWeight={700}
            className="text-slate-500"
            fill="currentColor"
          >
            X
          </text>

          {TICKS.map((n) => {
            const x = 40 + n * 52;
            const isSel = selected.includes(n);
            return (
              <g
                key={n}
                onClick={() => handleTickClick(n)}
                className="cursor-pointer"
              >
                <line
                  x1={x}
                  y1={50}
                  x2={x}
                  y2={70}
                  className="text-slate-500"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth={n === 0 ? 3 : 2}
                />
                <circle
                  cx={x}
                  cy={60}
                  r={isSel ? 11 : 16}
                  fill={isSel ? '#6366F1' : 'transparent'}
                  className="hover:fill-indigo-400/35 transition"
                />
                {isSel && <circle cx={x} cy={60} r={5} fill="#ffffff" />}
                <text
                  x={x}
                  y={95}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  fontSize={14}
                  fontWeight={isSel ? 800 : 600}
                  className={isSel ? undefined : 'text-slate-700'}
                  fill={isSel ? '#6366F1' : 'currentColor'}
                >
                  {n}
                </text>
                {n === 0 && (
                  <text
                    x={x}
                    y={38}
                    textAnchor="middle"
                    fontSize={12}
                    fontWeight={700}
                    className="text-slate-500"
                    fill="currentColor"
                  >
                    O
                  </text>
                )}
              </g>
            );
          })}
        </svg>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold min-h-[20px]">
          {feedback && (
            <span className={feedback.ok ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'}>
              {feedback.text}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        {mode === 'inequality' && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={checkInequality}
            className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold shadow-xs transition"
          >
            Проверить ответ
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/DiagramsSim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import MathInput from '@/components/ui/MathInput';
import { randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

interface Props {
  onResult: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

interface SliceItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  color: string;
}

type DiagramTask =
  | {
      kind: 'angle_from_percent';
      percent: number;
      answer: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: 'percent_from_angle';
      angle: number;
      answer: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: 'bar_percent';
      items: { label: string; value: number; color: string }[];
      targetIdx: number;
      total: number;
      answer: number;
    };

const PALETTE = ['#4F46E5', '#10B981', '#F59E0B', '#EC4899', '#0EA5E9'];

const INITIAL_DIAGRAM_TASK: DiagramTask = {
  kind: 'angle_from_percent',
  percent: 25,
  answer: 90,
};

const PRESETS: { name: string; items: Omit<SliceItem, 'id' | 'color'>[] }[] = [
  {
    name: 'Кружки 5 класса (30 уч.)',
    items: [
      { label: 'Футбол', value: 12 },
      { label: 'Шахматы', value: 6 },
      { label: 'Рисование', value: 9 },
      { label: 'Робототехника', value: 3 },
    ],
  },
  {
    name: 'Оценки за СОР (20 уч.)',
    items: [
      { label: 'Отлично', value: 6 },
      { label: 'Хорошо', value: 10 },
      { label: 'Удовлетв.', value: 4 },
    ],
  },
  {
    name: 'Режим дня (24 ч.)',
    items: [
      { label: 'Сон', value: 9 },
      { label: 'Школа', value: 6 },
      { label: 'Уроки и кружки', value: 4 },
      { label: 'Отдых', value: 5 },
    ],
  },
];

const CLEAN_PERCENTS = [10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50, 60, 75];
const CLEAN_ANGLES = [36, 54, 72, 90, 108, 144, 180, 216, 270];

function buildPreset(idx: number): SliceItem[] {
  return PRESETS[idx].items.map((item, i) => ({
    id: `s-${idx}-${i}`,
    label: item.label,
    value: item.value,
    color: PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length],
  }));
}

function makeDiagramTask(): DiagramTask {
  const roll = randInt(0, 2);
  if (roll === 0) {
