    const percent = CLEAN_PERCENTS[randInt(0, CLEAN_PERCENTS.length - 1)];
    return {
      kind: 'angle_from_percent',
      percent,
      answer: (percent * 360) / 100,
    };
  }
  if (roll === 1) {
    const angle = CLEAN_ANGLES[randInt(0, CLEAN_ANGLES.length - 1)];
    return {
      kind: 'percent_from_angle',
      angle,
      answer: Math.round((angle / 360) * 100),
    };
  }
  const total = randInt(0, 1) === 0 ? 20 : 25;
  const labels = ['Яблоки', 'Груши', 'Бананы', 'Сливы'];
  const v0 = randInt(3, 8);
  const v1 = randInt(3, 7);
  const v2 = randInt(2, 6);
  const v3 = total - v0 - v1 - v2;
  const values = [v0, v1, v2, v3];
  const targetIdx = randInt(0, 3);
  return {
    kind: 'bar_percent',
    items: labels.map((label, i) => ({
      label,
      value: values[i],
      color: PALETTE[i],
    })),
    targetIdx,
    total,
    answer: (values[targetIdx] * 100) / total,
  };
}

function PieSvg({
  slices,
  highlightOnlyFirst = false,
  firstLabel,
}: {
  slices: { label: string; value: number; color: string }[];
  highlightOnlyFirst?: boolean;
  firstLabel?: string;
}) {
  const total = slices.reduce((s, item) => s + Math.max(0, item.value), 0);
  const CX = 110;
  const CY = 110;
  const R = 92;

  if (total <= 0) {
    return (
      <svg viewBox="0 0 220 220" className="w-52 h-52 sm:w-56 sm:h-56 shrink-0">
        <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r={R} fill="rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25)" />
        <text
          x={CX}
          y={CY + 4}
          textAnchor="middle"
          fontSize={12}
          className="text-slate-500"
          fill="currentColor"
          fontWeight={700}
        >
          Нет данных
        </text>
      </svg>
    );
  }

  const computedSlices: {
    color: string;
    startDeg: number;
    sliceDeg: number;
    pct: number;
  }[] = [];

  let currentDeg = 0;
  for (const s of slices) {
    const val = Math.max(0, s.value);
    if (val === 0) continue;
    const sliceDeg = (val / total) * 360;
    computedSlices.push({
      color: s.color,
      startDeg: currentDeg,
      sliceDeg,
      pct: Math.round((val / total) * 100),
    });
    currentDeg += sliceDeg;
  }

  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 220 220" className="w-52 h-52 sm:w-56 sm:h-56 shrink-0 select-none">
      {computedSlices.map((s, idx) => {
        const { startDeg, sliceDeg, pct } = s;
        const midRad = ((startDeg + sliceDeg / 2 - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
        const lx = CX + 56 * Math.cos(midRad);
        const ly = CY + 56 * Math.sin(midRad);

        if (sliceDeg >= 359.99) {
          return (
            <g key={idx}>
              <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r={R} fill={s.color} />
              <text
                x={CX}
                y={CY + 5}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fontSize={14}
                fontWeight={800}
                fill="#ffffff"
              >
                {firstLabel || '100%'}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        }

        const startRad = ((startDeg - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
        const endRad = ((startDeg + sliceDeg - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
        const x1 = CX + R * Math.cos(startRad);
        const y1 = CY + R * Math.sin(startRad);
        const x2 = CX + R * Math.cos(endRad);
        const y2 = CY + R * Math.sin(endRad);
        const largeArc = sliceDeg > 180 ? 1 : 0;

        const textLabel =
          highlightOnlyFirst && idx === 0
            ? firstLabel || `${pct}%`
            : highlightOnlyFirst
              ? ''
              : sliceDeg >= 28
                ? `${pct}%`
                : '';

        return (
          <g key={idx}>
            <path
              d={`M ${CX} ${CY} L ${x1.toFixed(1)} ${y1.toFixed(1)} A ${R} ${R} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2.toFixed(1)} ${y2.toFixed(1)} Z`}
              fill={s.color}
              stroke="var(--surface-card, #ffffff)"
              strokeWidth={2}
            />
            {textLabel && (
              <text
                x={lx}
                y={ly + 4}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fontSize={12}
                fontWeight={800}
                fill="#ffffff"
              >
                {textLabel}
              </text>
            )}
          </g>
        );
      })}
    </svg>
  );
}

export default function DiagramsSim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'explore' | 'trainer'>('explore');
  const [items, setItems] = useState<SliceItem[]>(() => buildPreset(0));

  const [task, setTask] = useState<DiagramTask>(INITIAL_DIAGRAM_TASK);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  function clearPendingTimer() {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => clearPendingTimer();
  }, []);

  const total = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Math.max(0, item.value), 0);
  const maxVal = Math.max(1, ...items.map((i) => i.value));

  function updateItem(id: string, patch: Partial<SliceItem>) {
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...patch } : item))
    );
  }

  function addCategory() {
    if (items.length >= 5) return;
    setItems((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      {
        id: `s-${Date.now()}`,
        label: `Группа ${prev.length + 1}`,
        value: 5,
        color: PALETTE[prev.length % PALETTE.length],
      },
    ]);
  }

  function removeCategory(id: string) {
    if (items.length <= 2) return;
    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  }

  function nextTask() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setTask(makeDiagramTask());
    setInput('');
    setFeedback(null);
  }

  function checkCurrentAnswer() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const cleaned = input.trim().replace(/[°%\s]/g, '').replace(',', '.');
    const val = Number(cleaned);
    if (!cleaned || Number.isNaN(val)) return;

    const ok = Math.abs(val - task.answer) < 1e-6;
    onResult(ok);

    if (ok) {
      const unit = task.kind === 'angle_from_percent' ? '°' : '%';
      setFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Точно! Ответ: ${task.answer}${unit}.`,
      });
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(nextTask, 1300);
    } else {
      let hint = '';
      if (task.kind === 'angle_from_percent') {
        hint = `Вспомни: весь круг = 360°, значит 1% = 3,6°. Умножь ${task.percent} на 3,6.`;
      } else if (task.kind === 'percent_from_angle') {
        hint = `Весь круг — 360° (100%). Раздели ${task.angle}° на 360° и умножь на 100% (или раздели на 3,6).`;
      } else {
        const chosen = task.items[task.targetIdx];
        hint = `Всего на диаграмме ${task.total} шт. Раздели ${chosen.value} на ${task.total} и умножь на 100%.`;
      }
      setFeedback({ ok: false, text: `✗ Неверно. ${hint}` });
    }
  }

  function handleCheck(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    checkCurrentAnswer();
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              clearPendingTimer();
              setMode('explore');
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'explore' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Конструктор диаграмм
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setMode('trainer');
              nextTask();
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'trainer' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Тренажёр: Градусы и проценты
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === 'explore' ? (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {PRESETS.map((p, idx) => (
              <button
                key={p.name}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setItems(buildPreset(idx))}
                className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 transition"
              >
                {p.name}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={nextTask}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition"
          >
            ↻ Новое задание
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'explore' ? (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {/* Круговая диаграмма */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-slate-500">
                Круговая диаграмма (360° = 100%)
              </div>
              <PieSvg slices={items} />
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Сумма значений: <strong className="text-slate-900">{total}</strong> (1 единица ≈{' '}
                <strong>{total > 0 ? (360 / total).toFixed(1) : '0'}°</strong>)
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Столбчатая диаграмма */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-slate-500 text-center">
                Столбчатая диаграмма
              </div>
              <div className="h-48 flex items-end justify-around gap-3 px-2 pt-6 pb-1 border-b-2 border-slate-200">
                {items.map((item) => {
                  const pctHeight = Math.round((Math.max(0, item.value) / maxVal) * 100);
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-end h-full gap-1 min-w-0"
                    >
                      <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-700">
                        {item.value}
                      </span>
                      <div
                        className="w-full max-w-[56px] rounded-t-xl transition-all duration-300"
                        style={{
                          height: `${Math.max(4, pctHeight)}%`,
                          backgroundColor: item.color,
                        }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-around gap-2 text-center">
                {items.map((item) => (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className="flex-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 truncate"
                    title={item.label}
                  >
                    {item.label}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Таблица данных с расчётом процентов и градусов */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-slate-500">
                Данные и расчёт секторов
              </div>
              {items.length < 5 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={addCategory}
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold transition"
                >
                  + Добавить сектор
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              {items.map((item) => {
                const pct = total > 0 ? ((item.value / total) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                const deg = total > 0 ? ((item.value / total) * 360).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 items-center p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs"
                  >
                    <div className="col-span-5 sm:col-span-4 flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                      <span
                        className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: item.color }}
                      />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={item.label}
                        onChange={(e) => updateItem(item.id, { label: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-2 py-1 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg font-semibold text-slate-800"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 sm:col-span-3 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          updateItem(item.id, { value: Math.max(0, item.value - 1) })
                        }
                        className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 font-bold hover:bg-slate-100"
                      >
                        −
                      </button>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min={0}
                        max={200}
                        value={item.value}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          updateItem(item.id, {
                            value: Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value) || 0),
                          })
                        }
                        className="w-12 py-1 text-center font-extrabold bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => updateItem(item.id, { value: item.value + 1 })}
                        className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 font-bold hover:bg-slate-100"
                      >
                        +
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-3 sm:col-span-4 flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-1.5 font-bold">
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700">
                        {pct}%
                      </span>
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-800">
                        {deg}°
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-1 text-right">
                      {items.length > 2 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => removeCategory(item.id)}
                          className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 font-bold"
                          title="Удалить"
                        >
                          ✕
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 border border-slate-200 space-y-5">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 items-center">
            <div className="flex justify-center">
              {task.kind === 'angle_from_percent' && (
                <PieSvg
                  highlightOnlyFirst
                  firstLabel={`${task.percent}%`}
                  slices={[
                    { label: 'Сектор', value: task.percent, color: '#4F46E5' },
                    {
                      label: 'Остальное',
                      value: 100 - task.percent,
                      color: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25)',
                    },
                  ]}
                />
              )}
              {task.kind === 'percent_from_angle' && (
                <PieSvg
                  highlightOnlyFirst
                  firstLabel={`${task.angle}°`}
                  slices={[
                    { label: 'Сектор', value: task.angle, color: '#10B981' },
                    {
                      label: 'Остальное',
                      value: 360 - task.angle,
                      color: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25)',
                    },
                  ]}
                />
              )}
              {task.kind === 'bar_percent' && (
                <div className="w-full max-w-xs space-y-2">
                  <div className="h-44 flex items-end justify-around gap-3 px-2 pt-5 pb-1 border-b-2 border-slate-200">
                    {task.items.map((it, idx) => {
                      const maxBar = Math.max(...task.items.map((x) => x.value));
                      const h = Math.round((it.value / maxBar) * 100);
                      const isTarget = idx === task.targetIdx;
                      return (
                        <div
                          key={it.label}
                          className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-end h-full gap-1"
                        >
                          <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-800">
                            {it.value}
                          </span>
                          <div
                            className={`w-full max-w-[44px] rounded-t-xl transition ${
                              isTarget ? 'ring-2 ring-offset-2 ring-indigo-500' : 'opacity-65'
                            }`}
                            style={{ height: `${h}%`, backgroundColor: it.color }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-around gap-1 text-center">
                    {task.items.map((it, idx) => (
                      <span
                        key={it.label}
                        className={`flex-1 text-[11px] truncate ${
                          idx === task.targetIdx
                            ? 'font-extrabold text-indigo-700'
                            : 'font-semibold text-slate-500'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {it.label}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-4">
              {task.kind === 'angle_from_percent' && (
                <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                  Выделенный сектор составляет{' '}
                  <span className="text-indigo-700">{task.percent}%</span> круга. Чему равна
                  его градусная мера (в градусах)?
                </p>
              )}
              {task.kind === 'percent_from_angle' && (
                <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                  Градусная мера выделенного сектора равна{' '}
                  <span className="text-emerald-700">{task.angle}°</span>. Сколько это
                  процентов от всего круга?
                </p>
              )}
              {task.kind === 'bar_percent' && (
                <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                  Сколько процентов от всех фруктов ({task.total} шт.) составляют{' '}
                  <span className="text-indigo-700">
                    {task.items[task.targetIdx].label.toLowerCase()}
                  </span>{' '}
                  ({task.items[task.targetIdx].value} шт.)?
                </p>
              )}

              <form onSubmit={handleCheck} className="flex gap-2">
                <MathInput
                  value={input}
                  onChange={setInput}
                  onSubmit={checkCurrentAnswer}
                  placeholder={
                    task.kind === 'angle_from_percent' ? 'Например: 90' : 'Например: 25'
                  }
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-base font-bold bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                />
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold transition shrink-0"
                >
                  Проверить
                </button>
              </form>

              {feedback && (
                <div
                  className={`p-3 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold border ${
                    feedback.ok
                      ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200'
                      : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {feedback.text}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/DivisibilitySim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

interface Props {
  onResult: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

const DIVISORS = [2, 3, 5, 9, 10];
const POOLS = [
  12, 15, 18, 20, 24, 27, 30, 36, 45, 54, 60, 72, 75, 90, 105, 120, 135, 144,
  180, 210, 225, 234, 270, 315, 360, 405, 450, 512, 630, 729, 810, 945, 1230,
  2340, 137, 251,
];

function pickNumber() {
  return POOLS[randInt(0, POOLS.length - 1)];
}

export default function DivisibilitySim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const [num, setNum] = useState(() => pickNumber());
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);
  const [isOk, setIsOk] = useState(false);

  const digitSum = String(num)
    .split('')
    .reduce((s, d) => s + Number(d), 0);
  const correctSet = DIVISORS.filter((d) => num % d === 0);

  function toggleDivisor(d: number) {
    if (checked) return;
    setSelected((prev) =>
      prev.includes(d) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== d) : [...prev, d]
    );
  }

  function handleCheck() {
    const chosen = [...selected].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const ok =
      correctSet.length === chosen.length &&
      correctSet.every((v, i) => v === chosen[i]);
    setIsOk(ok);
    setChecked(true);
    onResult(ok);
  }

  function nextCard() {
    setNum(pickNumber());
    setSelected([]);
    setChecked(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto text-center space-y-5">
      <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-600">
        Блиц-тренажёр · Выбери ВСЕ верные делители числа
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl py-7 px-6 border-2 border-indigo-100 shadow-xs">
        <div className="text-5xl sm:text-6xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
          {num}
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">
          Если число не делится ни на один из делителей — оставь кнопки пустыми и нажми «Проверить»
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-2 sm:gap-3">
        {DIVISORS.map((d) => {
          const isSel = selected.includes(d);
          let cls = isSel
            ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600 shadow-md scale-[1.03]'
            : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300';

          if (checked) {
            const actuallyDiv = num % d === 0;
            if (actuallyDiv && isSel) cls = 'bg-emerald-500 text-white border-emerald-600';
            else if (actuallyDiv && !isSel) cls = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border-amber-400 ring-2 ring-amber-300';
            else if (!actuallyDiv && isSel) cls = 'bg-rose-500 text-white border-rose-600';
          }

          return (
            <button
              key={d}
              type="button"
              disabled={checked}
              onClick={() => toggleDivisor(d)}
              className={`py-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 font-extrabold text-base sm:text-lg transition ${cls}`}
            >
              ÷ {d}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {checked && (
        <div
          className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl text-xs sm:text-sm text-left leading-relaxed border ${
            isOk
              ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 border-emerald-200'
              : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-900 border-rose-200'
          }`}
        >
          <strong>{isOk ? '✓ Отлично!' : '✗ Есть неточность.'}</strong> Сумма цифр числа {num} равна{' '}
          <strong>{digitSum}</strong>, последняя цифра — <strong>{num % 10}</strong>. Верные делители:{' '}
          <strong>{correctSet.length ? correctSet.join(', ') : 'нет среди 2, 3, 5, 9, 10'}</strong>.
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex justify-center">
        {!checked ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCheck}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold shadow-xs transition"
          >
            Проверить
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={nextCard}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-sm font-bold shadow-xs transition"
          >
            Следующее число →
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/FractionsSim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { gcd, lcm, randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

interface Props {
  onResult: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

const INITIAL_PAIR = {
  a: { n: 2, d: 3 },
  b: { n: 3, d: 5 },
};

function makeComparePair() {
  const d1 = randInt(2, 9);
  const d2 = randInt(2, 9);
  return {
    a: { n: randInt(1, d1 - 1), d: d1 },
    b: { n: randInt(1, d2 - 1), d: d2 },
  };
}

function PizzaCircles({ n, d }: { n: number; d: number }) {
  const count = Math.min(3, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(n / d)));
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
      {Array.from({ length: count }, (_, c) => {
        const filled = Math.max(0, Math.min(d, n - c * d));
        return (
          <svg key={c} viewBox="0 0 120 120" className="w-28 h-28 sm:w-32 sm:h-32 shrink-0">
            {d === 1 ? (
              <circle
                cx={60}
                cy={60}
                r={52}
                fill={filled > 0 ? 'var(--color-indigo-500, #6366F1)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.28)'}
                stroke="var(--surface-card, #ffffff)"
                strokeWidth={2}
              />
            ) : (
              Array.from({ length: d }, (_, i) => {
                const a1 = (i / d) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
                const a2 = ((i + 1) / d) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
                const x1 = 60 + 52 * Math.cos(a1);
                const y1 = 60 + 52 * Math.sin(a1);
                const x2 = 60 + 52 * Math.cos(a2);
                const y2 = 60 + 52 * Math.sin(a2);
                const largeArc = 1 / d > 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
                return (
                  <path
                    key={i}
                    d={`M60,60 L${x1.toFixed(1)},${y1.toFixed(1)} A52,52 0 ${largeArc},1 ${x2.toFixed(1)},${y2.toFixed(1)} Z`}
                    fill={i < filled ? 'var(--color-indigo-500, #6366F1)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.28)'}
                    stroke="var(--surface-card, #ffffff)"
                    strokeWidth={2}
                  />
                );
              })
            )}
          </svg>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function FractionsSim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'explore' | 'compare'>('explore');
  const [num, setNum] = useState(6);
  const [den, setDen] = useState(8);

  const [pair, setPair] = useState(INITIAL_PAIR);
  const [cmpFeedback, setCmpFeedback] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  function clearPendingTimer() {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => clearPendingTimer();
  }, []);

  const g = gcd(num, den);
  const isProper = num < den;
  const whole = Math.floor(num / den);
  const rem = num % den;

  function nextCompare() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setPair(makeComparePair());
    setCmpFeedback(null);
  }

  function handleCompare(sign: '<' | '=' | '>') {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const left = pair.a.n * pair.b.d;
    const right = pair.b.n * pair.a.d;
    const trueSign = left < right ? '<' : left > right ? '>' : '=';
    const commonD = lcm(pair.a.d, pair.b.d);
    const n1 = pair.a.n * (commonD / pair.a.d);
    const n2 = pair.b.n * (commonD / pair.b.d);
    const ok = sign === trueSign;

    onResult(ok);
    if (ok) {
      setCmpFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Верно! Общий знаменатель ${commonD}: ${n1}/${commonD} ${trueSign} ${n2}/${commonD}.`,
      });
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(nextCompare, 1400);
    } else {
      setCmpFeedback({
        ok: false,
        text: `✗ Неверно. Приведи к знаменателю ${commonD}: получим ${n1}/${commonD} и ${n2}/${commonD}.`,
      });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            clearPendingTimer();
            setMode('explore');
          }}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
            mode === 'explore' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
          }`}
        >
          Конструктор и сокращение
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setMode('compare');
            nextCompare();
          }}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
            mode === 'compare' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
          }`}
        >
          Сравнение дробей
        </button>
      </div>

      {mode === 'explore' ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 items-center">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
            <PizzaCircles n={num} d={den} />
            <div className="w-full space-y-1">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 text-center">
                Полоска (1 целая разделена на {den} ч.)
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-7 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-300 flex bg-slate-100">
                {Array.from({ length: den }, (_, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={i}
                    className={`flex-1 border-r last:border-r-0 border-white/60 transition-colors ${
                      i < Math.min(num, den) ? 'bg-indigo-500' : 'bg-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                  <span className="text-slate-700">Числитель (сколько взяли):</span>
                  <span className="text-indigo-600 text-sm">{num}</span>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={1}
                  max={den * 2 + 4}
                  value={num}
                  onChange={(e) => setNum(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full accent-indigo-600 cursor-pointer"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                  <span className="text-slate-700">Знаменатель (на сколько разделили):</span>
                  <span className="text-indigo-600 text-sm">{den}</span>
                </div>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={1}
                  max={12}
                  value={den}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const nextDen = Number(e.target.value);
                    setDen(nextDen);
                    if (num > nextDen * 2 + 4) setNum(nextDen * 2 + 4);
                  }}
                  className="w-full accent-indigo-600 cursor-pointer"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-100 space-y-2 text-xs sm:text-sm">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Вид дроби:</span>
                <span className={`font-bold ${isProper ? 'text-emerald-700' : 'text-amber-700'}`}>
                  {isProper ? 'Правильная (< 1)' : 'Неправильная (≥ 1)'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Сокращение (НОД = {g}):</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-indigo-800">
                  {num}/{den} {g > 1 ? `= ${num / g}/${den / g}` : '(несократимая)'}
                </span>
              </div>
              {!isProper && (
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Смешанное число:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {rem === 0 ? whole : `${whole} и ${rem / g}/${den / g}`}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-5">
          <p className="text-center text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
            Сравни две дроби, глядя на доли или приведя к общему знаменателю:
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 items-center">
            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-indigo-700">
                {pair.a.n}/{pair.a.d}
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-8 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-300 flex">
                {Array.from({ length: pair.a.d }, (_, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={i}
                    className={`flex-1 border-r last:border-r-0 border-white/60 ${
                      i < pair.a.n ? 'bg-indigo-500' : 'bg-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              {(['<', '=', '>'] as const).map((sign) => (
                <button
                  key={sign}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleCompare(sign)}
                  className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-600 hover:text-white text-xl font-extrabold transition"
                >
                  {sign}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <div className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-700">
                {pair.b.n}/{pair.b.d}
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-8 rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-300 flex">
                {Array.from({ length: pair.b.d }, (_, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={i}
                    className={`flex-1 border-r last:border-r-0 border-white/60 ${
                      i < pair.b.n ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {cmpFeedback && (
            <div
              className={`text-center text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold ${
                cmpFeedback.ok ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'
              }`}
            >
              {cmpFeedback.text}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="text-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={nextCompare}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 transition"
            >
              ↻ Другая пара дробей
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/PercentGridSim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import MathInput from '@/components/ui/MathInput';
import { gcd, randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

interface Props {
  onResult: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

type GridTask =
  | {
      kind: 'paint_decimal';
      target: number;
      label: string;
    }
  | {
      kind: 'paint_fraction';
      target: number;
      label: string;
    }
  | {
      kind: 'read_percent';
      target: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: 'read_decimal';
      target: number;
    };

const FRACTION_TASKS: { n: number; d: number; pct: number }[] = [
  { n: 1, d: 2, pct: 50 },
  { n: 1, d: 4, pct: 25 },
  { n: 3, d: 4, pct: 75 },
  { n: 1, d: 5, pct: 20 },
  { n: 2, d: 5, pct: 40 },
  { n: 3, d: 5, pct: 60 },
  { n: 4, d: 5, pct: 80 },
  { n: 1, d: 10, pct: 10 },
  { n: 3, d: 10, pct: 30 },
  { n: 7, d: 10, pct: 70 },
  { n: 9, d: 10, pct: 90 },
  { n: 1, d: 20, pct: 5 },
  { n: 3, d: 20, pct: 15 },
  { n: 7, d: 20, pct: 35 },
  { n: 9, d: 20, pct: 45 },
];

const DECIMAL_POOL = [5, 8, 10, 12, 15, 20, 24, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50, 60, 65, 75, 80, 90];

const INITIAL_GRID_TASK: GridTask = {
  kind: 'paint_decimal',
  target: 25,
  label: '0,25',
};

function formatDecimal(p: number): string {
  if (p === 0) return '0';
  if (p === 100) return '1';
  return String(Number((p / 100).toFixed(2))).replace('.', ',');
}

function makeCells(count: number): boolean[] {
  return Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => i < count);
}

function makeTask(): GridTask {
  const roll = randInt(0, 3);
  if (roll === 0) {
    const pct = DECIMAL_POOL[randInt(0, DECIMAL_POOL.length - 1)];
    return {
      kind: 'paint_decimal',
      target: pct,
      label: formatDecimal(pct),
    };
  }
  if (roll === 1) {
    const item = FRACTION_TASKS[randInt(0, FRACTION_TASKS.length - 1)];
    return {
      kind: 'paint_fraction',
      target: item.pct,
      label: `${item.n}/${item.d}`,
    };
  }
  const pct = DECIMAL_POOL[randInt(0, DECIMAL_POOL.length - 1)];
  return roll === 2
    ? { kind: 'read_percent', target: pct }
    : { kind: 'read_decimal', target: pct };
}

export default function PercentGridSim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'explore' | 'trainer'>('explore');
  const [cells, setCells] = useState<boolean[]>(() => makeCells(25));
  const [dragValue, setDragValue] = useState<boolean | null>(null);

  const [task, setTask] = useState<GridTask>(INITIAL_GRID_TASK);
  const [answerInput, setAnswerInput] = useState('');
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  function clearPendingTimer() {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    function stopDrag() {
      setDragValue(null);
    }
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', stopDrag);
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', stopDrag);
    return () => {
      clearPendingTimer();
      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', stopDrag);
      window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', stopDrag);
    };
  }, []);

  const filledCount = cells.filter(Boolean).length;
  const g = filledCount > 0 ? gcd(filledCount, 100) : 1;

  const isReadTask =
    mode === 'trainer' && (task.kind === 'read_percent' || task.kind === 'read_decimal');

  function startNewTask() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const next = makeTask();
    setTask(next);
    setAnswerInput('');
    setFeedback(null);
    if (next.kind === 'read_percent' || next.kind === 'read_decimal') {
      setCells(makeCells(next.target));
    } else {
      setCells(makeCells(0));
    }
  }

  function switchMode(nextMode: 'explore' | 'trainer') {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setMode(nextMode);
    setFeedback(null);
    if (nextMode === 'explore') {
      setCells(makeCells(25));
    } else {
      startNewTask();
    }
  }

  function handleCellDown(idx: number) {
    if (isReadTask) return;
    const nextVal = !cells[idx];
    setDragValue(nextVal);
    setCells((prev) => prev.map((v, i) => (i === idx ? nextVal : v)));
  }

  function handleCellEnter(idx: number) {
    if (isReadTask || dragValue === null) return;
    setCells((prev) =>
      prev[idx] === dragValue ? prev : prev.map((v, i) => (i === idx ? dragValue : v))
    );
  }

  function toggleRow(rowIdx: number) {
    if (isReadTask) return;
    const start = rowIdx * 10;
    const rowAllFilled = cells.slice(start, start + 10).every(Boolean);
    setCells((prev) =>
      prev.map((v, i) => (i >= start && i < start + 10 ? !rowAllFilled : v))
    );
  }

  function handleCheckPaint() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const ok = filledCount === task.target;
    onResult(ok);
    if (ok) {
      setFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Верно! ${
          task.kind === 'paint_decimal' || task.kind === 'paint_fraction'
            ? `${task.label} = `
            : ''
        }${task.target}% (${task.target} клеток из 100).`,
      });
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(startNewTask, 1300);
    } else {
      setFeedback({
        ok: false,
        text: `✗ Сейчас закрашено ${filledCount} кл. (${filledCount}%). Вспомни: 100% — это весь квадрат из 100 клеток.`,
      });
    }
  }

  function checkReadAnswer() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const cleaned = answerInput
      .trim()
      .replace(/\s+/g, '')
      .replace(/−/g, '-')
      .replace('%', '')
      .replace(',', '.');
    const num = Number(cleaned);
    if (!cleaned || Number.isNaN(num)) return;

    const ok =
      task.kind === 'read_percent'
        ? Math.abs(num - task.target) < 1e-6
        : Math.abs(num - task.target / 100) < 1e-6;

    onResult(ok);
    if (ok) {
      setFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Отлично! ${task.target} из 100 клеток — это ${task.target}% и десятичная дробь ${formatDecimal(task.target)}.`,
      });
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(startNewTask, 1300);
    } else {
      setFeedback({
        ok: false,
        text:
          task.kind === 'read_percent'
            ? '✗ Неверно. Посчитай число закрашенных клеток: 1 полный ряд = 10%, 1 клетка = 1%.'
            : '✗ Неверно. Чтобы перевести проценты в десятичную дробь, раздели число клеток на 100 (сдвинь запятую на 2 знака влево).',
      });
    }
  }

  function handleCheckRead(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    checkReadAnswer();
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('explore')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'explore' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Сетка 10×10 (Конструктор)
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('trainer')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'trainer' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Тренажёр: Проценты и дроби
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === 'trainer' && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={startNewTask}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition"
          >
            ↻ Новое задание
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'trainer' && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200 text-center">
          {task.kind === 'paint_decimal' && (
            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
              Закрась на сетке 10×10 долю, равную десятичной дроби{' '}
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700">
                {task.label}
              </span>
            </p>
          )}
          {task.kind === 'paint_fraction' && (
            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
              Закрась на сетке 10×10 долю, равную обыкновенной дроби{' '}
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700">
                {task.label}
              </span>
            </p>
          )}
          {task.kind === 'read_percent' && (
            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
              Сколько <span className="text-indigo-700">процентов</span> квадрата закрашено на сетке?
            </p>
          )}
          {task.kind === 'read_decimal' && (
            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
              Запиши закрашенную часть квадрата в виде{' '}
              <span className="text-indigo-700">десятичной дроби</span>:
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 items-center">
        {/* Левая колонка: интерактивная сетка 10×10 */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 border border-slate-200 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2 select-none">
            {!isReadTask && (
              <div className="grid grid-rows-10 gap-1 pt-0.5">
                {Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, r) => (
                  <button
                    key={r}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleRow(r)}
                    title={`Закрасить / снять ряд ${r + 1} (10%)`}
                    className="w-6 h-6 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 hover:text-indigo-700 transition"
                  >
                    +10
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="grid grid-cols-10 gap-1 p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 touch-none">
              {cells.map((filled, idx) => (
                <button
                  key={idx}
                  type="button"
                  disabled={isReadTask}
                  onPointerDown={() => handleCellDown(idx)}
                  onPointerEnter={() => handleCellEnter(idx)}
                  className={`w-6 h-6 sm:w-7 sm:h-7 rounded-[5px] border transition-colors ${
                    filled
                      ? 'bg-indigo-600 border-indigo-700'
                      : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300'
                  } ${isReadTask ? 'cursor-default' : 'cursor-pointer'}`}
                  aria-label={`Клетка ${idx + 1}`}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {!isReadTask && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full max-w-[320px] text-[11px] text-slate-500">
              <span>Кликай по клеткам или жми «+10» слева</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setCells(makeCells(0))}
                className="font-bold text-rose-600 hover:underline"
              >
                Очистить
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Правая колонка: перевод или ввод ответа */}
        {mode === 'explore' ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                <span className="text-slate-700">Закрашено клеток из 100:</span>
                <span className="text-indigo-600 text-sm">{filledCount}%</span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0}
                max={100}
                value={filledCount}
                onChange={(e) => setCells(makeCells(Number(e.target.value)))}
                className="w-full accent-indigo-600 cursor-pointer"
              />
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-2">
                {[0, 10, 20, 25, 50, 75, 100].map((v) => (
                  <button
                    key={v}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setCells(makeCells(v))}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-100 hover:text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold transition"
                  >
                    {v}%
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-100 space-y-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Проценты:</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-indigo-800">{filledCount}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Десятичная дробь:</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                  {formatDecimal(filledCount)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Обыкновенная дробь:</span>
                <span className="font-extrabold text-emerald-700">
                  {filledCount === 0
                    ? '0'
                    : filledCount === 100
                      ? '100/100 = 1'
                      : g > 1
                        ? `${filledCount}/100 = ${filledCount / g}/${100 / g}`
                        : `${filledCount}/100`}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between pt-2 border-t border-indigo-100">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Осталось до целого (100%):</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-700">{100 - filledCount}%</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
            {isReadTask ? (
              <form onSubmit={handleCheckRead} className="space-y-3">
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">
                  {task.kind === 'read_percent'
                    ? 'Введи число процентов (например: 35):'
                    : 'Введи десятичную дробь (например: 0,35):'}
                </label>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <MathInput
                    value={answerInput}
                    onChange={setAnswerInput}
                    onSubmit={checkReadAnswer}
                    placeholder={task.kind === 'read_percent' ? '35%' : '0,35'}
                    className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-base font-bold bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold transition shrink-0"
                  >
                    Проверить
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-3 text-center">
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
                  Сейчас закрашено клеток:
                </div>
                <div className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-indigo-700">
                  {filledCount} <span className="text-base font-bold text-slate-400">/ 100</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCheckPaint}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-sm font-bold transition"
                >
                  Проверить сетку
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {feedback && (
              <div
                className={`p-3 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold border ${
                  feedback.ok
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200'
                    : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-200'
                }`}
              >
                {feedback.text}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/ProtractorSim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

interface Props {
  onResult: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

const TARGET_PRESETS = [25, 35, 45, 60, 75, 90, 110, 125, 135, 150, 165, 180];
const CX = 180;
const CY = 180;
const R = 135;

function getAngleType(deg: number) {
  if (deg === 0) return { name: 'Нулевой угол', color: 'text-slate-600' };
  if (deg < 90) return { name: 'Острый угол (< 90°)', color: 'text-emerald-600' };
  if (deg === 90) return { name: 'Прямой угол (= 90°)', color: 'text-indigo-600' };
  if (deg < 180) return { name: 'Тупой угол (90° < α < 180°)', color: 'text-amber-600' };
  if (deg === 180) return { name: 'Развёрнутый угол (= 180°)', color: 'text-purple-600' };
  if (deg < 360) return { name: 'Больше развёрнутого', color: 'text-sky-600' };
  return { name: 'Полный угол (= 360°)', color: 'text-rose-600' };
}

export default function ProtractorSim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement | null>(null);
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const [angle, setAngle] = useState(60);
  const [taskMode, setTaskMode] = useState(false);
  const [targetAngle, setTargetAngle] = useState(120);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  function clearPendingTimer() {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => clearPendingTimer();
  }, []);

  function nextTask() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setTargetAngle(TARGET_PRESETS[randInt(0, TARGET_PRESETS.length - 1)]);
    setFeedback(null);
  }

  function updateFromPointer(clientX: number, clientY: number) {
    const svg = svgRef.current;
    if (!svg) return;
    const rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
    const px = ((clientX - rect.left) * 360) / rect.width - CX;
    const py = ((clientY - rect.top) * 250) / rect.height - CY;
    let deg = Math.round((-Math.atan2(py, px) * 180) / Math.PI);
    if (deg < 0) deg += 360;
    if (taskMode && deg > 180) deg = deg > 270 ? 0 : 180;
    setAngle(deg);
  }

  function checkAngle() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    const ok = Math.abs(angle - targetAngle) <= 3;
    onResult(ok);
    if (ok) {
      setFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Точно в цель! Ты построил ${angle}° (цель ${targetAngle}°).`,
      });
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(nextTask, 1400);
    } else {
      setFeedback({
        ok: false,
        text: `✗ Сейчас угол ${angle}°, а нужно ${targetAngle}°. Подправь луч!`,
      });
    }
  }

  const rad = (-angle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const x2 = CX + R * Math.cos(rad);
  const y2 = CY + R * Math.sin(rad);
  const arcR = 46;
  const ax = CX + arcR * Math.cos(rad);
  const ay = CY + arcR * Math.sin(rad);
  const largeArc = angle > 180 ? 1 : 0;
  const arcPath =
    angle === 360
      ? `M ${CX + arcR} ${CY} A ${arcR} ${arcR} 0 1 0 ${CX + arcR} ${CY + 0.1}`
      : `M ${CX} ${CY} L ${CX + arcR} ${CY} A ${arcR} ${arcR} 0 ${largeArc} 0 ${ax.toFixed(1)} ${ay.toFixed(1)} Z`;

  const info = getAngleType(angle);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              clearPendingTimer();
              setTaskMode(false);
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              !taskMode ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Свободный транспортир
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setTaskMode(true);
              setAngle(30);
              nextTask();
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              taskMode ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Задание: Построй угол
          </button>
        </div>

        {taskMode && (
          <span className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 text-xs font-extrabold border border-amber-300">
            🎯 Цель: построй угол {targetAngle}°
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5 items-center">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 border border-slate-200 flex justify-center">
          <svg
            ref={svgRef}
            viewBox="0 0 360 250"
            className="w-full max-w-[360px] h-auto select-none touch-none cursor-crosshair"
            onPointerDown={(e) => {
              e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
              setDragging(true);
              updateFromPointer(e.clientX, e.clientY);
            }}
            onPointerMove={(e) => {
              if (dragging) updateFromPointer(e.clientX, e.clientY);
            }}
            onPointerUp={() => setDragging(false)}
            onPointerCancel={() => setDragging(false)}
          >
            <path
              d={`M ${CX - R} ${CY} A ${R} ${R} 0 0 1 ${CX + R} ${CY} Z`}
              fill="rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.12)"
              stroke="rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.45)"
              strokeWidth={2}
            />
            <circle
              cx={CX}
              cy={CY}
              r={R}
              fill="none"
              stroke="rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3)"
              strokeWidth={1}
              strokeDasharray="4 4"
            />

            <g className="text-slate-500">
              {Array.from({ length: 37 }, (_, i) => i * 5).map((d) => {
                const tRad = (-d * Math.PI) / 180;
                const major = d % 10 === 0;
                const inner = major ? R - 14 : R - 8;
                return (
                  <g key={d}>
                    <line
                      x1={CX + inner * Math.cos(tRad)}
                      y1={CY + inner * Math.sin(tRad)}
                      x2={CX + R * Math.cos(tRad)}
                      y2={CY + R * Math.sin(tRad)}
                      stroke="currentColor"
                      strokeWidth={major ? 1.5 : 0.8}
                    />
                    {d % 30 === 0 && (
                      <text
                        x={CX + (R - 27) * Math.cos(tRad)}
                        y={CY + (R - 27) * Math.sin(tRad) + 4}
                        textAnchor="middle"
                        fontSize={11}
                        fontWeight={700}
                        fill="currentColor"
                      >
                        {d}°
                      </text>
                    )}
                  </g>
                );
              })}
            </g>

            <path
              d={arcPath}
              fill="#6366F1"
              fillOpacity={0.25}
              stroke="#6366F1"
              strokeWidth={1.5}
            />
            <g className="text-slate-800">
              <line
                x1={CX}
                y1={CY}
                x2={CX + R + 10}
                y2={CY}
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth={3}
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />
              <circle cx={CX} cy={CY} r={5} fill="currentColor" />
            </g>
            <line
              x1={CX}
              y1={CY}
              x2={x2}
              y2={y2}
              stroke="#6366F1"
              strokeWidth={3.5}
              strokeLinecap="round"
            />
            <circle cx={x2} cy={y2} r={10} fill="#6366F1" stroke="#ffffff" strokeWidth={3} />
          </svg>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
          <div className="text-center">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase">Градусная мера</div>
            <div className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1">
              {taskMode ? '???°' : `${angle}°`}
            </div>
            {!taskMode ? (
              <div className={`text-sm font-bold mt-1 ${info.color}`}>{info.name}</div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Вращай синий луч мышкой/пальцем или ползунком
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <input
              type="range"
              min={0}
              max={taskMode ? 180 : 360}
              value={angle}
              onChange={(e) => setAngle(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full accent-indigo-600 cursor-pointer"
            />
            <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold mt-1">
              <span>0°</span>
              <span>90°</span>
              <span>180°</span>
              {!taskMode && (
                <>
                  <span>270°</span>
                  <span>360°</span>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {!taskMode ? (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 justify-center">
              {[30, 45, 90, 120, 180, 270, 360].map((v) => (
                <button
                  key={v}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAngle(v)}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-100 hover:text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold transition"
                >
                  {v}°
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {feedback && (
                <div
                  className={`text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-center ${
                    feedback.ok ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  {feedback.text}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={checkAngle}
                  className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition"
                >
                  Проверить угол
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={nextTask}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 transition"
                >
                  ↻
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/SetsVennSim.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { randInt } from '@/lib/math/utils';

type SetOp = 'inter' | 'union' | 'A' | 'B' | 'diffAB' | 'diffBA';

interface Props {
  onResult?: (isCorrect: boolean) => void;
}

const SET_A_ONLY = [2, 4, 8];
const SET_BOTH = [6, 12];
const SET_B_ONLY = [3, 9, 15];

const OPS: Record<
  SetOp,
  { label: string; short: string; desc: string; elems: number[]; zones: string[] }
> = {
  inter: {
    label: 'A ∩ B (Пересечение)',
    short: 'A ∩ B',
    desc: 'Только общие элементы, принадлежащие И множеству A, И множеству B.',
    elems: SET_BOTH,
    zones: ['both'],
  },
  union: {
    label: 'A ∪ B (Объединение)',
    short: 'A ∪ B',
    desc: 'Все элементы из A и B вместе, без повторений.',
    elems: [...SET_A_ONLY, ...SET_BOTH, ...SET_B_ONLY].sort((a, b) => a - b),
    zones: ['a', 'both', 'b'],
  },
  A: {
    label: 'Множество A (чётные)',
    short: 'A',
    desc: 'Все элементы, входящие в круг A (включая общую область).',
    elems: [...SET_A_ONLY, ...SET_BOTH].sort((a, b) => a - b),
    zones: ['a', 'both'],
  },
  B: {
    label: 'Множество B (кратные 3)',
    short: 'B',
    desc: 'Все элементы, входящие в круг B (включая общую область).',
    elems: [...SET_BOTH, ...SET_B_ONLY].sort((a, b) => a - b),
    zones: ['both', 'b'],
  },
  diffAB: {
    label: 'A \\ B (Только A)',
    short: 'A \\ B',
    desc: 'Элементы, которые есть в A, но отсутствуют в B.',
    elems: SET_A_ONLY,
    zones: ['a'],
  },
  diffBA: {
    label: 'B \\ A (Только B)',
    short: 'B \\ A',
    desc: 'Элементы, которые есть в B, но отсутствуют в A.',
    elems: SET_B_ONLY,
    zones: ['b'],
  },
};

const TRAINER_OPS: SetOp[] = ['inter', 'union', 'diffAB', 'diffBA', 'A', 'B'];

interface VennTask {
  aOnly: number[];
  both: number[];
  bOnly: number[];
  all: number[];
  targetOp: SetOp;
  expected: number[];
}

function makeVennTask(): VennTask {
  const pool = Array.from({ length: 19 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  for (let i = pool.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = randInt(0, i);
    [pool[i], pool[j]] = [pool[j], pool[i]];
  }

  const aOnly = pool.slice(0, 3).sort((x, y) => x - y);
  const both = pool.slice(3, 5).sort((x, y) => x - y);
  const bOnly = pool.slice(5, 8).sort((x, y) => x - y);
  const all = [...aOnly, ...both, ...bOnly].sort((x, y) => x - y);

  const targetOp = TRAINER_OPS[randInt(0, TRAINER_OPS.length - 1)];
  let expected: number[] = [];

  if (targetOp === 'inter') expected = [...both];
  else if (targetOp === 'union') expected = [...all];
  else if (targetOp === 'A') expected = [...aOnly, ...both].sort((x, y) => x - y);
  else if (targetOp === 'B') expected = [...both, ...bOnly].sort((x, y) => x - y);
  else if (targetOp === 'diffAB') expected = [...aOnly];
  else expected = [...bOnly];

  return { aOnly, both, bOnly, all, targetOp, expected };
}

export default function SetsVennSim({ onResult }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'explore' | 'trainer'>('explore');
  const [op, setOp] = useState<SetOp>('inter');

  const [task, setTask] = useState<VennTask>(() => makeVennTask());
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  function clearPendingTimer() {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => clearPendingTimer();
  }, []);

  function nextTask() {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setTask(makeVennTask());
    setSelected([]);
    setChecked(false);
    setFeedback(null);
  }

  function switchMode(nextMode: 'explore' | 'trainer') {
    clearPendingTimer();
    setMode(nextMode);
    if (nextMode === 'trainer') {
      nextTask();
    }
  }

  function toggleElem(n: number) {
    if (checked) return;
    setSelected((prev) =>
      prev.includes(n) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== n) : [...prev, n]
    );
  }

  function handleCheck() {
    const chosen = [...selected].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const ok =
      chosen.length === task.expected.length &&
      chosen.every((v, idx) => v === task.expected[idx]);

    setChecked(true);
    onResult?.(ok);

    if (ok) {
      setFeedback({
        ok: true,
        text: `✓ Верно! ${OPS[task.targetOp].short} = { ${task.expected.join(', ')} }.`,
      });
      clearPendingTimer();
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(nextTask, 1400);
    } else {
      setFeedback({
        ok: false,
        text: `✗ Не совсем точно. ${OPS[task.targetOp].desc} Верный ответ: { ${task.expected.join(', ')} }.`,
      });
    }
  }

  const activeZones =
    mode === 'explore'
      ? OPS[op].zones
      : checked
        ? OPS[task.targetOp].zones
        : [];
  const zA = activeZones.includes('a');
  const zBoth = activeZones.includes('both');
  const zB = activeZones.includes('b');

  const leftNums = mode === 'explore' ? SET_A_ONLY : task.aOnly;
  const bothNums = mode === 'explore' ? SET_BOTH : task.both;
  const rightNums = mode === 'explore' ? SET_B_ONLY : task.bOnly;
  const cur = OPS[op];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('explore')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'explore' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Исследование операций
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('trainer')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition ${
              mode === 'trainer' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            Тренажёр: Собери множество
          </button>
        </div>

        {mode === 'trainer' && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={nextTask}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition"
          >
            ↻ Новое задание
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'explore' && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {(Object.entries(OPS) as [SetOp, (typeof OPS)[SetOp]][]).map(([k, v]) => (
            <button
              key={k}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOp(k)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition ${
                op === k
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600'
                  : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300'
              }`}
            >
              {v.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 items-center">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200 flex justify-center">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 360 220" className="w-full max-w-[360px] h-auto select-none">
            <defs>
              <clipPath id="clipCircleA">
                <circle cx={135} cy={110} r={82} />
              </clipPath>
            </defs>

            <circle
              cx={135}
              cy={110}
              r={82}
              fill={zA ? '#818CF8' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.14)'}
              fillOpacity={zA ? 0.42 : 1}
              stroke="#6366F1"
              strokeWidth={2.5}
              className={mode === 'explore' ? 'cursor-pointer' : undefined}
              onClick={() => mode === 'explore' && setOp('diffAB')}
            />
            <circle
              cx={225}
              cy={110}
              r={82}
              fill={zB ? '#34D399' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.14)'}
              fillOpacity={zB ? 0.42 : 1}
              stroke="#10B981"
              strokeWidth={2.5}
              className={mode === 'explore' ? 'cursor-pointer' : undefined}
              onClick={() => mode === 'explore' && setOp('diffBA')}
            />
            <circle
              cx={225}
              cy={110}
              r={82}
              clipPath="url(#clipCircleA)"
              fill={zBoth ? '#4F46E5' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22)'}
              fillOpacity={zBoth ? 0.72 : 1}
              className={mode === 'explore' ? 'cursor-pointer' : undefined}
              onClick={() => mode === 'explore' && setOp('inter')}
            />
            <circle
              cx={135}
              cy={110}
              r={82}
              fill="none"
              stroke="#6366F1"
              strokeWidth={2.5}
              pointerEvents="none"
            />
            <circle
              cx={225}
              cy={110}
              r={82}
              fill="none"
              stroke="#10B981"
              strokeWidth={2.5}
              pointerEvents="none"
            />

            <text x={72} y={34} fontSize={15} fontWeight={800} fill="#6366F1">
              A
            </text>
            <text x={278} y={34} fontSize={15} fontWeight={800} fill="#10B981">
              B
            </text>

            <g className="text-slate-800" pointerEvents="none">
              <text x={102} y={88} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={16} fontWeight={700} fill="currentColor">
                {leftNums[0]}
              </text>
              <text x={88} y={122} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={16} fontWeight={700} fill="currentColor">
                {leftNums[1]}
              </text>
              <text x={110} y={152} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={16} fontWeight={700} fill="currentColor">
                {leftNums[2]}
              </text>

              <text x={252} y={88} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={16} fontWeight={700} fill="currentColor">
                {rightNums[0]}
              </text>
              <text x={268} y={122} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={16} fontWeight={700} fill="currentColor">
                {rightNums[1]}
              </text>
              <text x={246} y={152} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={16} fontWeight={700} fill="currentColor">
                {rightNums[2]}
              </text>
            </g>

            <g className={zBoth ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-800'} pointerEvents="none">
              <text x={180} y={98} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={17} fontWeight={800} fill="currentColor">
                {bothNums[0]}
              </text>
              <text x={180} y={134} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={17} fontWeight={800} fill="currentColor">
                {bothNums[1]}
              </text>
            </g>
          </svg>
        </div>

        {mode === 'explore' ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 uppercase">Выбранная операция</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900">{cur.label}</div>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{cur.desc}</p>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 font-mono text-sm font-bold text-indigo-800">
              {'{ '}
              {cur.elems.join(', ')}
              {' }'}
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              Подсказка: кликай прямо по областям диаграммы Эйлера–Венна!
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-indigo-600">
                Выбери ВСЕ элементы множества
              </div>
              <div className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
                {OPS[task.targetOp].label}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {task.all.map((n) => {
                const isSel = selected.includes(n);
                const isExp = task.expected.includes(n);
                let cls = isSel
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600 shadow-xs'
                  : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300';

                if (checked) {
                  if (isExp && isSel) cls = 'bg-emerald-500 text-white border-emerald-600';
                  else if (isExp && !isSel)
                    cls = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border-amber-400 ring-2 ring-amber-300';
                  else if (!isExp && isSel) cls = 'bg-rose-500 text-white border-rose-600';
                }

                return (
                  <button
                    key={n}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={checked}
                    onClick={() => toggleElem(n)}
                    className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-xl border-2 text-sm font-extrabold transition ${cls}`}
                  >
                    {n}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {feedback && (
              <div
                className={`p-3 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold border ${
                  feedback.ok
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200'
                    : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-200'
                }`}
              >
                {feedback.text}
              </div>
            )}

            <div>
              {!checked ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCheck}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition"
                >
                  Проверить множество
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={nextTask}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition"
                >
                  Следующее задание →
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/simulators/SimulatorsLabClient.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import AppNav from '@/components/layout/AppNav';
import GamificationHeader from '@/components/gamification/GamificationHeader';
